[\[1a.1\]]@#/_/rgyud 'bum kha pa bzhugs so/_/
[\[1b\]]
[\[1b.1\]{D370}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_shrI badzra DA ka nA ma ma hA tan+t+ra rA dzA/_bod skad du/_rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes bya ba/_bcom ldan 'das thugs 
[\[1b.2\]]rje chen po 'phags pa spyan ras gzigs dbang phyug la phyag 'tshal lo/_/mkha' 'gro kun dngos sems dpa' ni/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog_/gsang ba mchog gi 
[\[1b.3\]]dgyes pa na/_/thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs/_/'di ni rang byung bcom ldan 'das/_lhag pa yi ni lha gcig nyid/_/'byung gnas sgra tshul nges 'byung rnams/_/dam 
[\[1b.4\]]tshig spyod pa'i spyod yul yin/_/'jig rten gsum du rnyed dka' ba/_/thog ma dbus mthar yang dag gnas/_/'dod chags ma yin chags bral min/_/dbu mar yang ni mi 
[\[1b.5\]]dmigs so/_/bud med kun gyi sgyu ma'i rgya/_/'di ni gnyis med ye shes mchog_/rnyed dang bde ba kun mos pas/_/ngan par spyod pa'ang 'grub par 'gyur/_/nam mkha' 
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]kun tu rgyu grub pas/_/mkha' 'gro ma zhes rab tu grags/_/sna tshogs kun tu phyag rgya ni/_/sna tshogs kun gyi sdom pa yis/_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes byar grub/_/de nyid med sems rnal 
[\[2a.2\]]'byor pa/_/lha la dmigs par byed pa dag_/kun tu gzugs brnyan yid kyis ni/_/lugs sogs rnal 'byor skye ba min/_/byang chub sems can rnal 'byor gang /_/rnal 'byor dbang phyug rang nyid gtso/_/byang chub sems 'di rdo rje ste/_/sangs 
[\[2a.3\]]rgyas kun gyi rang bzhin no/_/'gro ba'i rtsa lag rang bzhin gyis/_/sna tshogs g.yogs te rnam bzhugs pas/_/de phyir 'bad pa thams cad kyis/_/sna tshogs tshul gyis mchod par bya/_/bdag nyid sangs rgyas thams cad dang /_/dpa' bo 
[\[2a.4\]]thams cad nyid yin te/_/rang gi lha yi sbyor ba yis/_/bdag nyid rab tu bsgrub par bya/_/'di yis sangs rgyas thams cad dang /_/dpa' bo thams cad nyid dang ni/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa thams cad du/_/tshe 'di nyid du 'grub 'gyur zhes/_/mkha' 'gro 
[\[2a.5\]]kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/dpal ldan bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs/_/thams cad nam mkha'i mtshan nyid de/_/nam mkha' la ni mtshan nyid med/_/
[\[2a.6\]]khams gsum pa ni ma lus pa'i/_/'gro kun sgyu ma lta bu yin/_/brtan pa dang ni g.yo ba yi/_/'gro ba dmigs su med pa ste/_/mthong ba dang ni reg pa'i dngos/_/thams cad sgyu ma ji bzhin no/_/gang gis blun po 'ching 'gyur ba/_/'dir 
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]des mkhas rnams rnam par grol/_/phyag rgya'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni/_/sa gsum ma lus bza' bar bya/_/gzhan du gal te sbyor ba ni/_/ma tshang mar me lta bur 'da'/_/gzugs ni sangs rgyas rnam snang mdzad/_/tshor ba rdo rje nyi ma yin/_/'du shes pad+ma gar 
[\[2b.2\]]dbang ste/_/'du byed rdo rje rgyal po yin/_/rnam shes rdo rje sems dpa' ste/_/'di dag dngos kun rang bzhin ni/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes byar grags/_/yang dag chen po gcig pu'i lha/_/bde ba thams cad rab ster ba/_/sgyu ma chen po'i rang bzhin gyis/_/
[\[2b.3\]]chags bral chags pa zhes byar bshad/_/sa khams ltung byed ma yin te/_/de bzhin chu khams gsod ma 'o/_/me khams 'gugs byed ma yin te/_/de bzhin rlung ni gar dbang ngo /_/nam mkha'i khams ni sgyu ma che/_/rang nyid 'gro ma zhes byar bshad/_/sangs rgyas 
[\[2b.4\]]kun dang mnyam sbyor ba/_/mkha' 'gro ma ni 'bar ba'i mchog_/'byung ba lnga yi rang bzhin gyis/_/de ni rdo rje gtso mor gnas/_/mig gnyis gti mug rdo rjer bshad/_/nyan pa zhe sdang rdo rjer brjod/_/sna ni ser sna rdo rje ste/_/kha ni 'dod chags rdo rjer gsungs/_/
[\[2b.5\]]lus ni phrag dog rdo rje ste/_/skye mched thams cad mnyam sbyor ba/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro ba de ba'i mchog_/gzugs 'di thams cad stong pa nyid/_/stong pa'i tshul gyi mig gis ni/_/dogs pa med par bsgom pa'i mchog_/rnam par mi rtog pa yis blta/_/nam mkha'i 
[\[2b.6\]]tshul gyi sems kyis ni/_/der ni thams cad nam mkha'i dngos/_/dogs pa med pa'i bsgom par ldan/_/rnam par mi rtog skye 'gyur zhes/_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs 
[\[2b.7\]]pa thams cad kyi thogs pa med cing gdon mi za ba bde ba chen po'i dam tshig ces bya ba'i rim pa'i ting nge 'dzin la snyoms par zhugs nas 'di skad ces gsungs so/_/ji srid 'khor ba'i gnas su ni/_/mkhas mchog 'dug par gyur pa'i bar/_/de srid sems can 
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]mtshungs med don/_/mya ngan mi 'da' bar ni mdzad/_/de ltar sems can dam pa rnams/_/sred pa'i zhags pas 'jigs med par/_/mi skyo bar ni brtson 'grus kyis/_/rtag tu sems can don rnams mdzad/_/nam mkha'i gnas kyi mthar thug par/_/
[\[3a.2\]]smon lam rnam par spyod byed cing /_/sems can don brtson sems kyis ni/_/dpa' bo srid pa sbyong bar mdzad/_/rtag tu 'dod pas myos pa yi/_/lha yi bu 'di rnams dang ni/_/gzhan yang skye bo chags can phyir/_/'dod chags thams cad dag par mdzad/_/bde chen 
[\[3a.3\]]shin tu grub bya'i phyir/_/rtog pa rgyas pa sbyin pa dang /_/thog ma tha ma med pa'i mchog_/rtog pa thams cad dang por bshad/_/'das pa'i dus na sngon byung ba/_/rtog pa dang po bde ba che/_/der ni rdo rje sems chen gyi/_/lhag pa yi ni lha gcig bzhugs/_/'jig 
[\[3a.4\]]rten khams rnams mtha' yas kyi/_/sems can kun don 'byung bya'i phyir/_/glu dang rol mo sogs pa'i gar/_/srid pa kun gyi gtso bor gyur/_/sems can thams cad 'byung ba yi/_/dga' ba yang dag bsdus pa nga /_/de nas sems can skye 'gyur ba'i/_/
[\[3a.5\]]'dod pa'i chags pa rnam spangs te/_/des 'dul bar ni mkhyen nas su/_/yi ge las byung rnams spangs shing /_/nam mkha' lta bur bdag med par/_/dag pa nyid du de nas son/_/de nas sems can sangs rgyas kyi/_/bla med don ni nus med pa'i/_/stong pa nyid 
[\[3a.6\]]la rtag bzhugs pas/_/'gro kun stong pas bcom nas ni/_/thams cad dmyal bar ltung mthong ste/_/slar yang bdag dang gzhan don la/_/mos pas gus pa nyid du gyur/_/de nas 'jig rten khams kun na/_/bzhugs pa'i sangs rgyas kun bsdus te/_/
[\[3a.7\]]myur ba nyid du bdag la bskul/_/oM ma hA su kha badzra satwa dzaHhU~M baM hoHsu ra ta s+t+waM/_/'dis ni bskul ba tsam gyis kyang /_/dag pa'i bde ba kun ster ba/_/'dod pa'i don kun sgrub byed cing /_/'gro kun bde ba skyed par gyur/_/nga ni 'dod pa'i gzugs kyi mchog_/
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]sangs rgyas kun gyis byin brlabs pa/_/nga nyid chos kyi sku yi mchog_/sangs rgyas kun gyi bdag po yin/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi sbyor 'phrog cing /_/sangs rgyas kun gyi bde ba che/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi sku chen po/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi gsung snyan pa/_/
[\[3b.2\]]sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs chen yin/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi mchod pa che/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi rgyal po che/_/rdo rje 'dzin pa kun gyi bdag_/'jig rten dbang phyug kun gyi gtso/_/rin chen 'dzin pa kun gyi dbang /_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor bas/_/sangs 
[\[3b.3\]]rgyas kun dang mnyam par 'gyur/_/sangs rgyas kun dang mnyam pas na/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes byar 'grub/_/dkyil 'khor chen po'i sbyor ba yis/_/de nas dkyil 'khor thams cad bri/_/de yi dbus su tshul bzhin du/_/rdo rje sems dpa' gzhag par bya/_/zla ba'i 
[\[3b.4\]]mdog can 'dzum dang bcas/_/'od zer dmar po 'khrugs pa las/_/sangs rgyas phreng bas mdzes pa ste/_/rgyan kun gyis ni rnam par bklubs/_/zhal bzhi phyag bzhi spyan gsum dang /_/ral pa'i thor tshugs kyis brgyan cing /_/lha mo bzhi dang yang dag ldan/_/
[\[3b.5\]]sangs rgyas gzugs kyis nye bar ba rgyan/_/rtsa zhal zhi zhing g.yas dkar min/_/de bzhin gzhan yang g.yon pa dmar/_/rgyab zhal ser po gser bsregs 'dra/_/thod pa la bzhugs gar dang ldan/_/sum cu gsum pa 'jigs par mdzad/_/rdo rje dang ni ral gri 
[\[3b.6\]]dang /_/dril bu thod pa bsnams pa nyid/_/mkha' 'gro ma yi 'khor los gang /_/skyob pa sna tshogs bri bar bya/_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde mchog zhes/_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis 
[\[3b.7\]]gsungs/_/ma dang sring mo bu mo dang /_/de bzhin nye du mo rnams ni/_/dngos grub thams cad rab sgrub pas/_/'dod pa'i sbyor bas chags par bya/_/ba glang sha dang rta sha sogs/_/dogs pa med bar bza' bar bya/_/'jig rten la rtag g.yon la spyod/_/
[\[4a\]]
[\[4a.1\]]brtan pa dang ni g.yo ba yi/_/'gro kun g.yon las byung bas na/_/g.yon gyis bza' zhing g.yon mchod bya/_/pho nya ma gtogs gzhan rnams la/_/'dod la rmongs pas bgrod mi bya/_/pho nya mo rtag zhen thar par/_/'gyur ba nyid du the tshom med/_/
[\[4a.2\]]sgrub pos mchog tu 'bad byas te/_/brtul zhugs min spangs brtul zhugs rdzogs/_/dam tshig rigs la spyod pa dang /_/tsa ru'i bza' ba lhan cig bya/_/rigs lnga la ni kun spyod par/_/rigs gcig nyid du brtag par bya/_/sbrang rtsi khrag dang ga pur bcas/_/
[\[4a.3\]]tsan+dan dmar po sbyar byas la/_/rdo rje chu dang bsres pa ni/_/dngos grub thams cad sgrub byed pa'i/_/de dag bdud rtsi lngar ni 'gyur/_/mthe bo srin lag sbyar ba yis/_/rnal 'byor rig pas rtag tu bza'/_/zhi ba'i btung ba bzhin myangs na/_/dngos grub rtag par 
[\[4a.4\]]'thob par 'gyur/_/'di dag dam tshig dam pa ste/_/mdzes ma'i gdong can khyod la bshad/_/las rnams kun la thabs bcas shing /_/dogs pa med par gang spyod dang /_/rnam par mi rtog sgom pa ni/_/brtul zhugs rnams kyi mchog gi mchog_/las 
[\[4a.5\]]rnams kun la kun tu ni/_/rnam par mi rtog sgo ma byed cing /_/dogs pa med par kun du spyod/_/de ni dka' thub rnams kyi mchog_/the tshom med pa'i sems kyis ni/_/skra zla la sogs rjes dngos kyi/_/yul rnams ma lus sten byed pa'i/_/dka' thub 
[\[4a.6\]]shin tu 'da' dka' ba'o/_/gang yang las rnams thams cad la/_/shes rab dang ni sbyar byas te/_/de yang stong pa'i gnas su sbyar/_/de 'dir bdag chen dka' thub yin/_/rnam par mi rtog sems kyis ni/_/shes rab dang ni mtshungs gzugs la/_/
[\[4a.7\]]dogs med spyod pa'i kun spyod pa/_/de ni bdag chen dka' thub yin/_/shes rab thabs ni ma gtogs par/_/gang tshe sems gzhan mi g.yo na/_/de tshe byang chub chen po ni/_/nges par ster ba nyid du bshad/_/rnam par mi rtog rjes 'jug la/_/
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]rnal 'byor pa ni mngon par brtson/_/de ni thams cad 'byung ba yi/_/shes rab pha rol phyin pa yin/_/gang tshe 'dus byas tshul rnams ni/_/rnam par mi rtog sems kyis su/_/me long lta bur rab shes pa/_/de ni shes rab bsten pa yin/_/glog dang chu bur 
[\[4b.2\]]ltar smin pa/_/gang tshe 'dus byas tshul rnams ni/_/gang gis ji ltar mthong 'gyur ba/_/de ni shes rab bsten pa yin/_/me long nang gi gzugs brnyan dang /_/rmi lam sgyu ma dri za'i grong /_/mig 'phrul mtshungs par gang gis mthong /_/de ni gtso bo yin par 
[\[4b.3\]]brjod/_/de ni gang du 'ang mngon zhen dang /_/gang du'ang gnas pa yod ma yin/_/de ltar yang dag 'byung mod kyi/_/sgyu ma lta bu'i dngos dag ni/_/longs spyod khyad 'phags skye 'gyur ba/_/bde ba yongs su 'gyur bzhin pas/_/'bras bu khyad 'phags 'thob 
[\[4b.4\]]'gyur bar/_/ci yi phyir na 'dod mi bya/_/brtan g.yo'i dngos po yod min te/_/'on kyang snang ba tsam mtshan nyid/_/bsgom pa khyad par can gang zhig_/goms pa'i stobs ni rdzogs pa na/_/yid 'ong bde ba'i 'bras bu ni/_/bla med gsal ba'i blo yi mthu/_/
[\[4b.5\]]'bras bu don gnyer rgyu bsgrubs na/_/der ni lha mo su zhig 'gog_/de bas 'bad pa thams cad kyis/_/kun tu 'gro ba mchod par bya/_/dka' thub sdom pa mi bzad pas/_/lus ni bskams shing sdug bsngal 'gyur/_/sdug bsngal gyis ni sems g.yengs 
[\[4b.6\]]na/_/g.yengs pas dngos grub gzhan du 'gyur/_/lus dang sems ni brtan gyur na/_/bde ba thams cad brtan par 'gyur/_/sdug bsngal gyis ni g.yo gyur pas/_/shin tu 'gog par 'gro bar 'gyur/_/des na lus dang ngag sems rnams/_/
[\[4b.7\]]brtan par byas na byang chub 'thob/_/gzhan du dus min 'chi ba dang /_/dmyal bar nges par bsreg 'gyur zhes/_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can 
[\[5a\]]
[\[5a.1\]]gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las de kho na nyid snang ba dam pa'i yul la 'jug pa'i ye shes kyi rim par phye ba dang po'o//_//de nas bcom ldan 'das la btsun mo rnams kyis slar yang mchod cing phyag byas nas 'di skad 
[\[5a.2\]]ces gsol to/_/bcom ldan 'das snying po gang gis de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bstan pa 'dral ba'i lha la sogs pa'i gdug pa can rnams dang /_skye bo gdug pa can gzhan dag kyang gdul ba dang /_'joms pa dang /_zhum par byed pa dang /_ltung ba dang /_zhi 
[\[5a.3\]]bar byas te/_byang chub kyi snying po'i bar du gnod pa byed par mi 'gyur ba'i snying po sdang ba thams cad rab tu 'joms pa lha yis bshad du gsol/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi sku dang /_gsung dang /_thugs rdo rje'i bdag pos/_glur blangs 
[\[5a.4\]]te 'di skad ces gsungs so/_/dang por rnam par snang mdzad nas/_/der ni sde tshan rnams kyi mthar/_/ta dang po yi d+ha b+ha'i mtha'/_/dbyangs kyi gsum pas spyir brgyan pa'i/_/sa yi mtha' ni rang gis blta/_/la sa'i dbus kyi stan bzhugs pa'i/_/sa yi mtha' ni sbyar 
[\[5a.5\]]bar bya/_/mtha' gnyis kyis ni rnam par sbyang /_/a stan gnas pa sa yi mtha'/_/slar yang sa mtha' mda' lnga bstan/_/zla ba'i dum bu thig les brgyan/_/la sa'i dbus gnas sa bon ni/_/mda' bcu yis ni yongs su sbyangs/_/sha dang sa yi dbus gnas la/_/
[\[5a.6\]]zhal gsum gyis ni brgyan pa dang /_/rlung gi phyogs gnas sa bon la/_/ru ta yis ni rnam par brgyan/_/hU~M hU~M ha ni bzung nas su/_/dbyangs kyi dgu pas yang dag dgang /_/phaT ces bya ba dgod par bya/_/lhag ma ha yi rkang pa dgang /_/lnga ba'i dbus gnas de nyid de/_/
[\[5a.7\]]de la ha dzo zhes bya'o/_/ra stan la gnas sa yi mtha'/_/i zhes bya ba'i mda' dang sbyar/_/slar yang pa zhes bya ba'i mtha'/_/mda' ni sar bas yongs su sbyangs/_/sa mtha' der ni ra yig zhon/_/e zhes bya bar rna ba'i rgyan/_/i dbyangs zla dum thig les brgyan//
[\[5b\]]
[\[5b.1\]]yi ge ra zhon sa yi mtha'/_/slar yang sa mthar sgra lngar ldan/_/zla dum thig les cod pan brgyan/_/slar yang de nyid mgon po bzla/_/rin chen mtha' ma phaT kyis brgyan/_/rnam par snang mdzad sna tshogs kyis/_/tshig rnams kun la dgang bya zhes/_/rdo rje sems 
[\[5b.2\]]dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/dpa' bo drug dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/mkha' 'gro ma yi dra ba'i sdom/_/sna tshogs pad+ma'i nyi dkyil la/_/der ni hU~M las byung ba yi/_/las kyi rdo rje'i dbus gnas par/_/hU~M gi de nyid rnam bsgom pa'o/_/
[\[5b.3\]]'od zer sna tshogs 'bar ba las/_/mkha' 'gro ma yi tshogs spro ba'o/_/zhal gcig phyag gnyis spyan gsum pa'i/_/sku mdog dmar mo drag chen mo/_/mdung gi dbyibs can 'jigs pa'i gzugs/_/spyan dmar kham skra gyen du brjes/_/lang tsho phun sum tshogs 
[\[5b.4\]]par ldan/_/stag gi pags pa'i na bza' can/_/ljags 'dril bas ni cher 'jigs te/_/'jigs pa dag kyang 'jigs par mdzad/_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro rab grags zhes/_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/
[\[5b.5\]]bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/sngon bsags las ni mthar byed pa/_/me dang bcas te lan gnyis bzla/_/ku ru ku ru sems dpa' bral/_/ral pa gya gyu ban d+ha gsal/_/de ni slar yang bzlas par bya/_/'jig rten gzugs rang bral ba yi/_/trA sa ya 
[\[5b.6\]]zhes bya ste [{]brdzun[,]rdzun[}]/_/dam pa de ni lan gnyis bzla/_/k+Sha zhes bya ba o sgrar ldan/_/glo na b+ha ya yang dag gnas/_/de yang slar ni bzlas par bya/_/sa yi mtha' ni nyi stan gnas/_/mda' bcu zla dum thig les dgang /_/de yang slar ni brjod par bya/_/'od byed sbyangs 
[\[5b.7\]]pa'i sa yi mtha'/_/gshegs su gsol ba glo na gnas/_/nyis 'gyur du ni bya ba yin/_/zla zhon thig gsal phe zhes bya/_/de yang slar ni bzlas par bya/_/phaT ces bya ba bsgyings te bsgrags/_/rnam par rtog pa kun gyi shing /_/da ha da ha mkha' 'gro'i sdom/_/
[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]lnga sde'i thog ma nyid dang ldan/_/sde gtso de ni slar bzlas bya/_/b+hak+Sha b+hak+Sha zhes bya dang /_/ba sa ru d+hi ra an+t+ra/_/mA lA pa laM bi ne dang /_/gr-ih+Na gr-ih+Na sa p+ta ste/_/pA ta la ga ta b+hu dza~M ga/_/sar+b+ba~M bA tardza ya nyis 'gyur bya/_/A dang ka yi yi ge dang /_/
[\[6a.2\]]gsum pa yi ni gsum par sbyar/_/bzhi pa dang ni rlung la gnas/_/lan gnyis su ni bzlas par bya/_/sa yi mtha' ni nyi mar gnas/_/yi ge bzhi pas dgang bar bya/_/slar yang de ni lan gnyis bzla/_/lnga sde mtha' yi stan bzhugs pa/_/ka Sha a dang thig ler 
[\[6a.3\]]ldan/_/sa bon de ni gnyis bzlas bya/_/sa mtha' mda' gnyis dang ldan pa/_/slar yang sa mtha' mda' gsum sbyar/_/de bzhin sa mtha' mda' lnga'i stan/_/de kun zla ba'i dum bu dang /_/thig le yis ni cod pan brgyan/_/de dag yang yang bsgyur bar bya/_/ka sa tsa 
[\[6a.4\]]dang d+ha rnams ni/_/i sgra yi ni mchog gis gzung /_/li yi mtha' ni re rer sbyar/_/de dag kyang ni yang yang bsgyur/_/u sgra la zhon sa yi mtha'/_/slar yang zla dum thig les sbyangs/_/phaT ces bya ba'i rin chen bsgrub/_/cha dang cha min sna tshogs pa'i/_/me tog 
[\[6a.5\]]mang pos yang dag zin/_/de dag lha mo sgyu ma che/_/dngos grub thams cad rab ster ba'o/_/'di ni gsungs pa tsam gyis kyang /_/'jig rten khams kun g.yos gyur cing /_/de bzhin gshegs pa kun skrag nas/_/slar yang de bzhin gshegs pa kun/_/byang chub tu 
[\[6a.6\]]ni mngon sangs rgyas/_/ri bo thams cad 'dar zhing gas/_/ma mo'i tshogs dang 'byung po'i tshogs/_/gdon dang rims dang dbyig dug dang /_/gnod sbyin srin po log 'dren sogs/_/sdang ba thams cad btul zhing bcom/_/zhi 'gyur bkug ste dkyil 'khor gyi/_/
[\[6a.7\]]dbus su bcug ste dam tshig gis/_/bcings nas kyang ni dbang du mdzad/_/dmyal ba pa yi sdig pa kun/_/dral te nges bcom ba snyil lo zhes/_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/
[\[6b\]]
[\[6b.1\]]bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa/_/sgo skyong dang ni yang dag ldan/_/de dbus pad+ma bri bya ba/_/'od zer dmar chen gsal ba'i mdangs/_/phyi rol ri mo zlum por bskor/_/de yi dbus su bcom ldan 'das/_/khro bo chen po zhal bzhis brgyan/_/

[\[13b.1\]]dgra bcings par 'gyur ba las gzhan du ma yin no/_/rat+na badzra zhes byas nas/_/de nas oM dang badzra brjod/_/hrI dang phaT ni mthar sbyar ba/_/sngags 'di dpung ni 'ching byed yin/_sa nag po la lha mo tsa mu N+DI gdong byas la/_go ro tsa nas bsgrub bya du yod pa'i ming dang spel ba'i

[\[14a.1\]]lta bu'i bdud rtsi'i chu rgyun 'babs pas a la khrus chab gsol ba rtag tu bsams na/_pad+ma kha sbyor gyi bsam gtan gyi sbyor ba 'dis ni lus brtan par 'gyur ba dang /_srog bsrung ba la snying gar dbang bskur zhing rgyas pa phun sum tshogs par 'gyur ro/_/

[\[14a.6\]]bya'o/_/bum pa dri'i chus bkang ste rin po che dang /_sman dang /_'bru dang ldan par byas shing stobs po che'i sngags brgya rtsa brgyad bzlas nas maN+Dal gru bzhi par byas pa'i steng du skyes bu'i gtso bo bzhag ste/_bum pa des khrus byas la sngags de gro ga la go ro tsa nas bris

[\[14b.1\]]lugs las bzlog ste bris la bcings nas nya phor gyi nang du bcug ste/_sha za'i shing gi rtsa ba'i rlung mtshams su sbas na ni bskrad par 'gyur la me mtshams su bzhag na ni phan tshun sdang bar 'gyur la/_bden bral gyi mtshams su gsang ste sbas na ni gsod par 'gyur

[\[14b.3\]]bsgrub bya'i ming bris la me mar gyi rtse mo la bsros na dbang du 'gyur ro/_/maN+Dal byugs te/_zan gyi chang bu la ming sngags bzlas nas dgongs mo tshang du 'gro kha ma'i bya rog la byin na yang bskrad par 'gyur ro/_/bsgegs nas bsad pa'i ro'i thag gu blangs la/_

[\[14b.5\]]na gang gi ming nas bzung ste bzlas pa de 'ong bar 'gyur ro/_/yang tha mal pa'i rdo'am/_brdar rdo la klu'i rgyal po gdengs ka gsum dang ldan pa'i gzugs byas la rdza ma nag po'i nang du bzhag ste/_sbrang rtsi dang 'bras yos kyi bo ga ling dang /_dri dang /_me tog dang /_

[\[15a\]]
[\[15a.1\]]grong brtan par bya ba'i sbyor ba gzhan yang rdza mkhan gyi lag pa phyis pa'i 'jim pa la snod byas la stong rtsa brgyad yongs su bzlas te/_dbugs mi 'byin par chu'i nang du bzhag na brtan par 'gyur ro/_/ji ste dgra gsad par 'dod na spra tshil lam/_bag zan las 
[\[15a.2\]]gzugs brnyan byas la/_chos gos kyi gzugs brnyan gyi gdong nas brtsams te rkang pa'i mthar thug par dkris la legs par sbas te/_de'i steng du lag pa bzhag la ming dang spel ba'i sngags stong du gcig gis ma tshang ba bzlas nas lha mchod pa'i khyim gyi phyogs gcig tu 
[\[15a.3\]]bzhag ste/_snying gar seng ldeng gi phur bu btab la phur bu'i steng du dus gsum du chu tshan blugs shing yongs su bzlas na brlag par 'gyur ro/_/nyi shu'i phyed kyi rdzas dang kha cher sbyar la rnal 'byor pas thig le byas na skye bo skal ba bzang por 'gyur ro/_/rdzas de dag ma la

[\[28b.1\]]gcod cing bud med thams cad la skal ba bzang por 'gyur la bzo dang nor thabs thams cad kyi bzo la mkhas pa ste/_tshe lo lnga bcu rtsa lnga'am/_yang na sum cu rtsa drug tu 'tsho la chu srin gyi nyi ma la 'chi bar the tshom med do/_/chu srin gyi nyi ma la btsas pa'i byis pa ni nyi ma bcu 
[\[28b.2\]]pa'am/_zla ba gsum pa'am/_lnga ba'am/_lo dgu pa la ma shi na bsod nams dang /_tshul khrims phun sum tshogs shing /_zhi des dang /_bslab pa dang ldan zhing nor dang /_gzugs dang /_skal ba phun sum tshogs la dpal dang grags pa dang /_bu dang /_tsha bo 'phel 
[\[28b.3\]]la/_grong khyer gyi gtso bo 'gyur ba dang /_'khor dang /_longs spyod phun sum tshogs pa dang /_bzo dang dus kyi mtha' la mkhas pa dang /_dgra thams cad rlag par byed do/_/tshe lo lnga bcu rtsa lnga'am/_yang na drug cu rtsa bzhir 'tsho la rus sbal gyi nyi ma la 'chi bar 'gyur ro/_/rus 
[\[28b.4\]]sbal gyi nyi ma la btsas pa'i byis pa ni nyi ma gnyis pa dang /_dgu pa dang /_zla ba brgyad pa dang /_dgu pa dang /_lo dgu pa la ma shi na de ni rtag tu sems khral che ba dang /_khro ba dang bcas shing cung zad kyis mgu ba dang /_/mang du smra zhing gnyen rnams byams pa dang /_rtag tu g.yem 
[\[28b.5\]]pa dang /_phyugs la gtong phod che ba dang /_bu dang chung ma gsod pa dang /_sems chung zhing bsam pa ngan pa dang /_zhing las kyis 'tsho zhing mdze nad kyis thebs te/_tshe lo drug cu rtsa bzhi'am/_brgyad cu tham par 'tsho ba khyu mchog gi nyi ma la rang gi yul du 'chi bar 'gyur ro/_/
[\[28b.6\]]khyu mchog gi nyi ma la btsas pa'i byis pa ni nyi ma dgu pa'am/_zla ba gnyis pa dang /_brgyad pa'am/_lo brgyad pa dang /_dgu pa la gal te ma shi na dpa' zhing rtul pho pas pha rol gnon la sgeg cing phyug pa dang /_chung ma mang du len la gnyen 'dun thams cad dga' zhing pha'i tshe 
[\[28b.7\]]'joms pa dang /_khe spogs dang /_g.yem pa la dga' zhing le lo dang /_mu cor du smra ba dang /_ngang tshul ngan pa dang /_las gdug pa la dga' ste/_tshe lo drug cu'am/_nyi shur 'tsho la bzang po'i nyi ma la mtshon gyis 'chi bar 'gyur ro/_/bzang po'i nyi ma la btsas pa'i 
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]byis pa ni nyi ma lnga pa'am/_zla ba dgu pa'am/_lo bcu pa la ma shi na de ni dbul zhing chos spyod la bden par smra ba dang /_snyan par smra ba dang /_chung ma gcig la dga' zhing rig pa'i bdag por 'gyur ba dang /_bzo dang /_dus thams cad shes shing rang gi gnyen 
[\[29a.2\]]'dun byams pa dang /_rigs kyi 'chad pas 'tsho ba ste/_tshe lo brgyar 'tsho la gti mug gi nyi ma la chur 'chi bar 'gyur ba las gzhan du ma yin no/_/gti mug gi nyi ma la btsas pa'i byis pa ni nyi ma gnyis pa'am/_zla ba dgu pa'am/_lo dgu pa la gal te shi chad du ma 
[\[29a.3\]]gyur na mang du za zhing sems chung la rtag tu g.yem pa la dga' ba dang /_rgyun du khro zhing brkam chags che la/_na dbu can du 'gyur ba dang /_yid gzhungs shing gnyen 'dun byams pa dang /_rtag tu khro ba dang nga rgyal can du 'gyur ba dang /_ngang tshul ngan zhing sdig spyod la tshig rtsub 
[\[29a.4\]]dang khyal ba smra ba la dga' ba dang /_mtshon cha rdeg pa la dga' ste/_tshe lo drug cu rtsa bzhir 'tsho la 'phel ba'i nyi ma la yan lag gi zug rngu dang skrangs 'bur gyis 'chi bar 'gyur ba la the tshom med do/_/'phel ba'i nyi ma la nad kyis thebs na shin tu bag bya dgos te/_gal te 
[\[29a.5\]]zhag [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]lnga la ma shi na de nas nyi ma lnga na 'tsho bar 'gyur ro/_/sa'i dbang phyug la ni nyi ma gsum na ma shi na de nas nyi ma bdun na 'tsho bar 'gyur ro/_/rmi lam la yang nyi ma gsum nam shi na de bzhin du nyi ma bdun na 'tsho'o/_/'phyo ba la ni nyi ma gsum mam lnga na 'chi 'tsho cha phyed 
[\[29a.6\]]do/_/'bugs pa la ni nyi ma gsum mam bzhi pa la ma shi na nyi ma bcu na 'tsho bar 'gyur ro/_/sme ba la ni nyi ma gsum pa la 'chi ba'am brgya pa la 'tsho ste/_yun ring du nad kyis gdung bar 'gyur ro/_/yugs sa mo la ni nyi ma lnga pa la 'chi ba'am dgu pa la ldang bar 
[\[29a.7\]]'gyur la/_zhag bcu na nad las grol bar 'gyur ro/_/chu srin la ni nyi ma brgyad pa la 'chi ba'am/_zhag nyi shu rtsa gcig gis 'tsho 'chi cha phyed par 'gyur ro/_/rus sbal la ni nyi ma brgyad pa la ma shi na des zhag bcu pa la nad las grol bar 'gyur ro/_/khyu mchog la ni nyi

[\[33a\]]
[\[33a.1\]]sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las/_/dbugs brtsi ba'i rim par phye ba zhes bya ba ste bcu pa'o//_//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba/_/dngos grub mchog gi rig ba la/_/

[\[33a.7\]]a ta sa yi me tog bzhin/_/'og tu bltas shing 'phyang ba ste/_/nyin dang mtshan du bdud rtsi yi/_/tshul gyis 'bab pas yang dag gnas/_/dang po 'khor lo bsgoms nas ni/_/dbyangs yig gis ni khyad par sbyar/_/rang gi phyag rgyar mngon ldan pas/_/sangs rgyas byang chub 
[\[33b\]]
[\[33b.1\]]rab tu ster/_/de yi brtul zhugs ma bshad de/_/sbyin sreg las dang sngags rnams dang /_/gzung ba dang ni bgrang phreng dang /_/gcig nas gcig tu ldog par 'gyur/_/sngags kyi dkyil 'khor dbyibs chos dag_/sbyor ba rnam gsum zhes bya ste/_/dug med byas nas nan tan

[\[33b.4\]]'od zer sngon po'i phreng 'khrugs las/_/'dam skyes sngon po lta bu yi/_/zhal gcig phyag bzhi spyan gsum pa/_/mgo skyes kham pa gyen bsgreng zhing /_/rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par bklubs/_/rked pa stag gi pags pas dkris/_/phyag na lcags kyu zhags pa dag_/mda'

[\[34a\]]
[\[34a.1\]]yin/_/b+ha ga yi ni dbus gyur pa/_/ye shes rgya mtsho bsgom byas la/_/yi ge mi 'gyur rdzogs pa las/_/sku mdog dkar po zhal bzhi pa/_/rdo rje dang ni kha T+wAM bsnams/_/dpa' bo sbyi gtsug zla phyed brgyan/_/gshor dang thod pa'ang bsnams pa ste/_/dbugs 
[\[34a.2\]]mi g.yo bar bsgom par bya/_/rlung dang me ni yang dag ldan/_/chu dang dbang chen mnan pa ste/_/spro ba'i bdag nyid can gyis spro/_/'bad de bsgrub bya dmigs na ni/_/khu ba dgug pa'i dam pa yin/_/tsi yi ye shes las byung ba/_/sku mdog ser po kun tu 'bar/_/zhal bzhi

[\[34b.1\]]byas nas ni 'di skad brjod/_/sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' rnams/_/kun nas bdag la dgongs gsol/_/bdag ming 'di zhes bgyi ba ni/_/dus 'di nas bzung ji srid du/_/byang chub snying por mchis kyi bar/_/ji ltar dus gsum mgon po rnams/_/yang dag byang chub nges mdzad 
[\[34b.2\]]pa/_/de bzhin bla med byang chub sems/_/dam pa bdag gis bskyed par bgyi/_/sangs rgyas rnal 'byor skyed sdom pa/_/sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun te/_/bla na med pa'i dkon mchog gsum/_/deng nas brtsams te mchog tu gzung /_/bslab pa yi ni tshul khrims dang /_/dge ba'i chos ni bsdu 
[\[34b.3\]]ba dang /_/sems can don byed tshul khrims gsum/_/bdag gis so sor brtan por bzung /_/rdo rje rigs mtho chen po la/_/rdo rje dril bu phyag rgya yang /_/yang dag nyid du gzung bgyi zhing /_/slob dpon dag kyang bdag gis gzung /_/rin chen rigs chen rnal 'byor gyi/_/

[\[34b.7\]]dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/sku mdog sngon po 'jigs chen po/_/thod pa'i phreng bas rnam par bklubs/_/zhal bzhi pa la phyag bzhi pa/_/spyan gsum gyis ni mdzes pa ste/_/[{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]chen po gdan du byas/_/nyi ma la bzhugs gar dang ldan/_/phyag na thod 
[\[35a\]]
[\[35a.1\]]pa kha T+wAM dang /_/de bzhin gzhan yang gzhu dang mda'/_/cho ga'i bya ba de nyid kyis/_/ye shes rgya mtsho bsgom par bya/_/shar ni dkar zhing 'od sngo ste/_/g.yon na kha T+wAM dril bu dang /_/gzhan na rdo rje thod pa'o/_/byang phyogs mar gad ser skya'i 'od/_/g.yon na kha TAM zhags pa 
[\[35a.2\]]dang /_/g.yas na rde'u chung thod pa bsnams/_/nub phyogs dmar zhing ser skya'i 'od/_/gzhu dang mda' yi mchog dang ni/_/sna tshogs pad+ma thod pa dag_/g.yon dang g.yas kyi phyag tu dgod/_/lho ni dkar zhing ser ba'i 'od/_/g.yon na dung dang rin chen te/_/g.yas na ba dan thod pa 
[\[35a.3\]]bsnams/_/glang chen ce spyang mi dang ni/_/glang gi thod par rim bzhin gzhug_/dang po ltung byed ma yin te/_/gnyis pa de bzhin gsod ma yin/_/gsum pa 'gugs ma zhes bya ste/_/bzhi pa gar gyi dbang phyug ma/_/rig ma de dag gyen skra 'bar/_/rgyan rnams thams cad rnam par

[\[35b\]]
[\[35b.1\]]hU~M bsgoms la/_/byams pa'i sems ni bskyed nas su/_/de nas dkyil 'khor bri bar bya/_/gru bzhi pa la sgo bzhi pa/_/kha khyer de bzhin rdo rjes mtshan/_/skud pa bzhi dang yang dag ldan/_/dar gyi phreng bas mdzes par byas/_/dkyil 'khor grwa rnams thams cad dang /_/sgo dang

[\[36a\]]
[\[36a.1\]]pa/_/mkha' 'gro ma ni de bzhin 'khor/_/glog lta bur ni gsal ba'i 'od/_/yid shugs chen pos rig pa che/_/lha yi rnal 'byor dus su ni/_/phyogs bcu rnams su spro bar bya/_/shin tu phra ba'i rdul tshogs ni/_/shin tu phra ba'i dngos por bsgom/_/'gyur

[\[36b.5\]]rab tu dbye ba dang /_/yul gyi dbye ba bshad par bya/_/go ni ma la ya yul te/_/spyi gtsug dzA laM d+ha ra yin/_/au D+yan rna ba g.yas pa ste/_/ar bu da ni rgyab phyogs yin/_/gnas kyi ming can rnam bzhi'o/_/go da ba ri rna g.yon te/_/rA me shwa ri smin dbus gsungs/_/mig

[\[37a.1\]]khyad par tshul gyis ni/_/sa spyod gsung gi 'khor lor gnas/_/mtshan ni [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]gnas pa ste/_/gr-i ha de ba bshang ba'i lam/_/de dag 'dun sar brjod pa ste/_/brla ni sau rASh+Ta zhes bya/_/byin pa gnyis ni gser gling ste/_/nye ba'i 'dun sa nyid yin

[\[37a.3\]]gnas su brjod/_/phyi dang nang dang yang dag gnas/_/nyi shu rtsa bzhir phye ba yis/_/sku gsung thugs kyi 'khor lo yin/_/gnas rnams dag ni thams cad du/_/rtsa rnams kun ni yang dag bshad/_/yul 'di rnams ni bu mos gang /_/rtsa yi gzugs kyis gnas so zhes/_/

[\[37a.5\]]dang rab tu gtum mo ste/_so dang sen mo 'bab pa'o/_/dzA laM d+ha ra ra keng rus chen po dang gtum mig ma ste/_/skra dang ba spu 'bab pa'o/_/au Di ya ne ra keng rus dang 'od ldan ma ste/_/pags pa dang dri ma 'bab pa'o/_/ar bu dar mche ba rnam par gtsigs pa dang sna chen ma ste/_

[\[37b.1\]]mig 'bab pa'o/_/o Ter ni rdo rje ral ba can dang 'jigs byed chen mo ste/_/mkhris pa 'bab par grags pa ste zhing ngo /_/tri sha ku ni ra dpa' bo chen po dang rlung gi shugs ma ste/_glo ba 'bab pa'o/_/ko sa lar rdo rje hU~M mdzad dang chang 'thung ma ste/_rgyu ma'i phreng ba 'bab pa'o/_/nye

[\[38a.1\]]pa'o//_//de nas las kyi rab mchog ni/_/sbyor ba kun gyi dam pa ste/_/gang yang grub par byed pa'i sngags/_/de yi dbye ba rim bzhin bshad/_/yi ge pho nya la sogs sngags/_/mda' yi shugs kyis yongs su 'khor/_/sprul pa'i 'khor

[\[41b.3\]]rgyal po blon bcas mdun na 'don/_/btsun mo'i 'khor dang bcas par ni/_/myur du 'gugs par byed pa yin/_/gro ga yi ni 'dab ma la/_/ro yi sen rtsi ro tsa nas/_/mi yi bdag po 'khor bcas pa/_/ming dang spel te bri bar bya/_/mngon par phyogs te bzlas bzhin 
[\[41b.4\]]du/_/seng ldeng dang ni dur khrod mer/_/ji srid spra tshil ma zhu bar/_/me yi steng du gdung bar bya/_/bsgrub bya mngon par gdungs tsam gyis/_/lha dang lha min 'ong 'gyur na/_/sa steng skye bo smos ci dgos/_/[{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]sen rtsi skyes pa yi/_/khrag dang gi wang bcas 
[\[41b.5\]]ba yis/_/bsgrub bya'i ming spel gro gar bri/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i rkang rjes sa/_/tsan+dan dmar po nyid dang ni/_/dur khrod zas lhag thal ba rnams/_/sgrub pa po yis gcig bsdus nas/_/seng ldeng me ni du med la/_/rdul de lag pa gnyis kyis ni/_/mnyes 
[\[41b.6\]]nas sbyin sreg byas pa yis/_/thal ba de ni bzung nas su/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i gzugs byas te/_/snying gar 'khrul 'khor bcug nas ni/_/zlos bzhin du ni mer gdungs na/_/brgyal zhing sems dang bral 'gyur la/_/rmongs shing rab tu myos nas ni/_/rlung 
[\[41b.7\]]gi shugs kyis 'ong bar 'gyur/_/cho ga'i bya ba 'di yis ni/_/yi ge nyi shu rtsa gnyis bzhed/_/lha mo sngags chen 'di dag ni/_/mkha' 'gro ma yi snying 'joms yin/_/de nas de bzhin gshegs kun gyi/_/yid 'ong zhi ba bskyed pa mo/_/bka' yi 'khor lo 
[\[42a\]]
[\[42a.1\]]bshad par bya/_/mkha' dbyings dbus gyur bsam pa ni/_/zla ba'i dkyil 'khor dam pa ste/_/rdo rje rtse gsum kha bzhi pa/_/des ni de nas bri bar bya/_/de dbus pad+ma 'dab brgyad pa/_/shin tu mdzes pa bris nas su/_/bai ro tsa na'i rig pa che/_/
[\[42a.2\]]sems can kun skyed sdud pa mo/_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/nad thams cad med par byed pa zhes bya ba'i srung ba'i 'khor lo'i sbyor ba'o/_/de nas bcom ldan kun mkhyen pas/_/ye shes mngon par shes pa yi 
[\[42a.3\]]'khor lo zhes bya bka' stsal pa/_/ge sar lte bar ldan pa yi/_/pad+ma 'dab ma drug par bri/_/zla ba bzang pos nye bar brgyan/_/rdo rje'i phreng bas rnam par bklubs/_/de yi phyi rol 'khor lo ste/_/ston ka'i zla ltar dri med pa/_/yi ge'i mchog ni rnam pa bdun/_/lha mo chen 
[\[42a.4\]]mo phan pa gcig_/bdud thams cad kyi las 'joms par byed pa 'khor lo'i rgyal po zhes bya'o/_/tsan+dan dkar po ro tsa nas/_/gro ga la ni bris nas su/_/'khor lo lag pa g.yon par bcang /_/'khor lo 'di ni lha mo che/_/las kyi 'od kyis brgyan pa yin/_/de nas 
[\[42a.5\]]bsgrub bya nyi dbus su/_/bsams te stong du bzlas nas ni/_/yi ge shel gyi rnam pa ltar/_/bsams te nad kyis gdungs la bstan/_/mthong ba tsam gyi mod la ni/_/nad rnams kun las rnam grol bar/_/'gyur ba nyid du the tshom med/_/lag pa g.yon par 'khor 
[\[42a.6\]]lo 'di/_/zla ba'i dbus su rnam bkod na/_/ston ka yi ni zla 'drar 'gyur/_/'khor lo 'di ni lha mo che/_/las kyi 'od kyis rab brgyan na/_/las kyi cho ga mthong ba yis/_/bya ba thams cad byed par 'gyur/_/dur khrod ras sam thod pa 'am/_/a rka yi ni 'dab ma la/_/
[\[42a.7\]]sngar bstan pa yi sol ba dang /_/skyes pa'i khrag gi snag tsha yis/_/sgrub pos mi rus smyug gus bri/_/nyi ma'i dbus gyur khro sems kyis/_/gzugs brnyan byas la bsgrub bya yi/_/dbus su de ni bcug nas su/_/ske tshe yis ni byugs nas ni/_/yang dang 
[\[42b\]]
[\[42b.1\]]yang du bsro bar bya/_/sngags kyi rgyal po bskul ba yis/_/bya ba thams cad byed par 'gyur/_/'di yi nad rnams thams cad ni/_/'phel dang de bzhin sbyor byed pa'o/_/rims la [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]snying gar bri/_/'ga' re [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]klad pa ste/_/brjed byed rlung dang sprin la bri/_/
[\[42b.2\]]gzer bar bya ba mdung la ste/_/dug dang mdze nad lto 'phye la/_/gza' la gza' yi mtshan mar bri/_/mdor bsdus nas ni 'khor lor dgod/_/shel dang zla ba'i 'od 'dra ba'i/_/yi ge bdud rtsi rgyun 'bab pas/_/ston ka'i zla zer lta bur ni/_/bsam gtan bsams pa 
[\[42b.3\]]tsam gyis ni/_/'chi ba'ang nges par slong bar 'gyur/_/sbrul chen brgya yis zin nas ni/_/'chi ba'ang myur du slong bar byed/_/dug gis brgyal bar gyur pa dag_/bstan pa tsam gyis slong bar 'gyur/_/rims dang gdon dang brjed byed dang /_/lag pa mthong bas rlag 
[\[42b.4\]]par byed/_/grib ma yang ni mi 'gom na/_/de yi lus lta smos ci dgos/_/zangs ma yi ni 'dab ma'am/_/lag pa g.yon par 'khor lo 'di/_/ba bla dang ni yung bas bri/_/de nas chu yi nang bzhag la/_/ji ltar 'byung bzhin 'bad de mchod/_/mtshams kyi dus su 
[\[42b.5\]]mnyam bzhag pas/_/de bzhin du ni sngags bzlas bya/_/chu yi nang nas bton nas su/_/'o mas bkrus la rgya mtshor gzhug_/g.yul du khu tshur bcangs nas ni/_/rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam brgyan te/_/yid la rtag tu zlos byed na/_/ji srid khu tshur ma bkrol bar/_/
[\[42b.6\]]myur du thams cad rengs 'gyur te/_/dgra bo gal te lag na ni/_/mtshon cha phyar te thogs pa yang /_/ji srid khu tshur ma bkrol bar/_/mtshon cha gtong bar mi nus so/_/yi ge bdun dang rnal 'byor ma/_/drug gi yi ge 'di dag ni/_/'khor lo drug la gnas par 
[\[42b.7\]]ni/_/lag pa'i dbus su bkod nas su/_/cho ga rim pas bcang bar bya/_/kha nas gsang sngags rgyal po zlos/_/de yi gzugs gyur bzung nas ni/_/de ni 'gugs 'gyur the tshom ma med/_/tsan+dan dmar po'i rdul dang ni/_/lan tshwa ske tshe de bzhin te/_/bsgrub 
[\[43a\]]
[\[43a.1\]]bya'i rkang rjes rdul dang ni/_/gcig tu byas nas btag par bya/_/lan brgyar bzlas te rab bsgoms na/_/sa bdag skye bo'i nor dang ni/_/bcas te dbang du 'gyur ba'am/_/de ni 'gugs 'gyur the tshom med/_/sbyor ba 'di ni grub pa ste/_/gzhan dag tu ni 
[\[43a.2\]]nga mi smra/_/rdul de nyid ni bzung nas su/_/lcags phye dang ni bsre bar bya/_/dur khrod ras la gtums nas su/_/lan bdun du ni bzlas par bya/_/lam gyi bzhi mdor sor brgyad tsam/_/brkos te dgra yi ming nas ni/_/bzung nas sa yis dgab par bya/_/gang gi ming 
[\[43a.3\]]bzung de bcings 'gyur/_/phye ma de nyid bzung nas su/_/'o ma yis ni 'bad de bkru/_/slar yang grol 'gyur the tshom med/_/phye ma de nyid bzung nas su/_/smyo byed lnga dang ldan par bya/_/ro yi gos la bsgrub bya yi/_/ming bris nas ni gtum par bya/_/lan brgya bzlas te 
[\[43a.4\]]dur khrod du/_/sbas na skyes bu 'phral du ni/_/smyo 'gyur 'di la the tshom med/_/phyung nas ba yi 'o ma yis/_/'bad de slar yang bkrus na ni/_/'grol 'gyur 'dir ni the tshom med/_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/de nas bcom 
[\[43a.5\]]ldan de bzhin gshegs/_/rdo rje dpa' bo'i 'khor lo gsungs/_/zla ba'i dbus 'gyur rdo rje ni/_/rtse gsum de bzhin kha drug pa/_/slar yang zla bar pad+ma ni/_/'dab drug legs par gnas pa bri/_/de yi dbus gyur sa bon ni/_/'dod don thams cad sgrub byed pa'o/_/
[\[43a.6\]]sngags pas lte bar bri bar bya/_/bsgrub bya'i ming dang spel bar bya/_/rdo rje sems sogs lha rnams ni/_/thams cad sna tshogs bla na med/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi go cha che/_/mdzes ma'i gdong can khyod la bshad/_/dbus su snga ma'i gsang sngags bri/_/pad+ma'i 'dab 
[\[43a.7\]]mar de bzhin gzhan/_/ring po drug ni yongs spangs pa/_/rdo rje yi ni lte bar bri/_/gur kum sen rtsi rang khrag dang /_/ro tsa na yis gro ga la/_/cho ga bzhin du bris nas ni/_/dus gsum du ni bzlas byas la/_/mchod chen byas nas gzung bar bya/_/lan bdun du ni 
[\[43b\]]
[\[43b.1\]]mngon bzlas te/_/gos kyi mtha' mar bcing bar bya/_/lam du 'gro na rkun po dang /_/stag sogs 'jigs par mi 'gyur ro/_/thams cad du ni bsrung 'gyur ba/_/'grub 'gyur 'di la the tshom med/_/lte ba der ni thig le yis/_/bskor ba'i thig le dag kyang bya/_/a ri tsa ta 
[\[43b.2\]]ka bcud dang /_/yung bdag gis khyad par du/_/a sho ka yi smyug gu yis/_/dpa' bo yi ni ras mar bri/_/bsgrub bya'i ming dang spel ba yis/_/sngags 'di stong du de nas bzla/_/bsgrub bya'i rkang rjes sa dang ni/_/glang po che yi rkang rjes rdul/_/dur khrod thal bar bcas pa las/_/
[\[43b.3\]]sgrub pos gzugs brnyan byas nas su/_/bcing bas bcings te de yi ni/_/snying ga ru ni gzhug par bya/_/me tog ser pos mchod nas ni/_/gsang ba'i gnas su sbas gyur na/_/dgra rnams thams cad 'ching 'gyur zhing /_/rlag par byed pa mi bzad par/_/rdo rje sems dpa' de 
[\[43b.4\]]bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/mkha' 'gro ma yi snying po'i sngags/_/de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs/_/nam mkha'i dbyings gyur sa bon ni/_/kun tu 'od zer rab 'phro ba/_/ston ka'i zla ltar dri med pa'i/_/'khor lo rtsibs ni bcu 
[\[43b.5\]]gnyis bya/_/rtsibs de rnams la sa bon gyi/_/yi ge mchog tu phan pa dgod/_/de yi lte bar dgod bya ba/_/rig pa chen mo'i yi ge che/_/bsrung ba rdo rje'i cho ga rig pas/_/mkha' 'gro ma kun go cha bya/_/sngags kyi rgyal po bskul ba yi/_/dbus su bsgrub bya'i ming bri bya/_/
[\[43b.6\]]rnam pa'i mchog kun ldan pa ru/_/spro ba'i bdag nyid can gyis spro/_/gur kum dang ni ro tsa nas/_/gro ga 'am ni ras dkar la/_/snying stobs can gyis bri bar bya/_/las kyi cho ga mthong ba yi/_/thams cad du ni bsrung ba dang /_/sgrub pa po yis sngar bstan bzhin/_/
[\[43b.7\]]gdug pa rnams ni tshar gcod byed/_/dngos grub thams cad rab ster ba'i/_/las mchog de nas bshad par bya/_/gang zhig bsgrubs pa tsam gyis ni/_/'jig rten gsum po dbang byed pa'o/_/skye gnyis rdo rje'i rigs dang ni/_/gzugs bzang bu mo gzhan yang rung /_/ka 
[\[44a\]]
[\[44a.1\]]ra bI ra'i me tog dmar/_/rgyal gyi sbyor bas bsgrub par bya/_/go ro tsa nas bsgrubs nas su/_/me tog der ni chur gnas la/_/sgrub po shin tu mnyam bzhag pas/_/lan brgya ru ni bzlas par bya/_/chu bo chen por zhugs nas su/_/lte ba man chad chur bcug ste/_/
[\[44a.2\]]lag pa thal sbyar dbus bzung nas/_/rig pa stong du bzlas par bya/_/snyim pa chu yis bkang ba dang /_/bcas pas sgrub pos btung bar bya/_/de nas de ni 'grub 'gyur te/_/sgrub pos ngag ni grub pa yis/_/rgyal po chung mar bcas pa dag_/yid kyis 'gugs 'gyur 
[\[44a.3\]]gdon mi za/_/ka ra bI ra'i me tog ni/_/dmar po yang dag bzung nas ni/_/rig pa lan bdun bzlas byas la/_/skye gnas dbus su gzhug bya ste/_/me tog de ni kha sbyar nas/_/gsang ba'i gnas su gzhag par bya/_/gnyis med ye shes rnal 'byor pas/_/brgya phrag 
[\[44a.4\]]gcig tu bzlas par bya/_/sgrub po rtse gcig yid kyis ni/_/me tog de ni blangs nas su/_/me tog gis ni gang bsnun pa/_/de yi mod la dbang du 'gyur/_/brgyal zhing sems dang bral ba dang /_/yan lag tshigs kun gzhan dbang ste/_/mngon 'gyur sems ni ldog 
[\[44a.5\]]pa ru/_/skad cig gis ni dbang byed 'gyur/_/'di ni mngon sum grub pa yi/_/don yod dbang du byed pa yin/_/zhes bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs so/_/rnal 'byor ma rnams 'gugs pa'i cho ga 'khor lo'i rgyal po zhes bya ba'o/_/gang zhig bris pa tsam gyis su/_/
[\[44a.6\]]rdo rje sems dpa' brlag 'gyur zhing /_/dngos grub myur du ster 'gyur ba/_/de nas yang dag bshad par bya/_/zla ba'i dbus gyur rdo rje ste/_/de bzhin gzhan yang pad+ma'o/_/kun nas rnam par 'phro ba yi/_/mkha' 'gro ma yi sa bon dgod/_/dbus su rdo rje phag mo 
[\[44a.7\]]ni/_/shin tu drag cing gsal ba'i gzugs/_/yi ge'i dgod pas dgod par bya/_/rim pa de ltar brjod pa la/_/zhi la zhi ba'i yid kyis su/_/ro tsa na yis gro gar bri/_/ma mo'i gnas dang dur khrod du/_/gos chen de bzhin khro dang bcas/_/chags pa la ni dmar 
[\[44b\]]
[\[44b.1\]]po'i gos/_/rgyas la gos ser de bzhin te/_/de dang der ni bsgom byas nas/_/ci rigs par ni sprul par bya/_/bu mo gzhon nu'i ro yi ni/_/gos ni 'bad de blangs nas su/_/tshes brgyad dang ni bcu bzhi la/_/de nyid las byung sol ba yis/_/sna tshogs rdo rje kha bzhi 
[\[44b.2\]]la/_/sngags dang spel bar bris nas ni/_/lan bdun du ni bzlas nas su/_/gang gi khyim du sbas gyur pa/_/de ni gshin rje'i grong khyer du/_/nyi ma bdun gyis 'gro bar 'gyur/_/de yi rkang pa'i rkang sbubs dang /_/bum pa la sogs dag kyang rung /_/rus pa'i phur bu'i sbu 
[\[44b.3\]]gur ni/_/'khrul 'khor 'di ni bcug bya ste/_/cho ga'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni/_/'chi 'gyur 'dir ni gdon mi za/_/las rnams kun la sgrub pa pos/_/cho ga dang ni ldan par bya/_/rang gis bzung ba'i sngags kyis ni/_/bskal pa bye ba brgyas mi 'grub/_/las 'di mchog tu 
[\[44b.4\]]shes bya ste/_/sngags gzhan rgyas pas ci zhig bya/_/de dag thams cad spangs nas su/_/'di nyid 'bad de gzung bar bya/_/gang dang gang gi bsam gtan gyis/_/sgrub pos bzlas pa brtsam par bya/_/sbyor ba de dang de dag gis/_/'dod pa'i 'bras bu 'thob par 
[\[44b.5\]]'gyur/_/ji ltar nor bu dag pa ni/_/rdzas gang la brten de gzugs 'dzin/_/de bzhin rdo rje mkha' 'gro yang /_/yang dag bsam gtan mchog 'dzin 'gyur/_/bsam pa gang dang gang gis ni/_/mi rnams yid ni yang dag sbyor/_/de dang de yi dngos 'gyur te/_/sna 
[\[44b.6\]]tshogs gzugs can nor bu bzhin/_/de nyid bsdus dang 'dus pa ste/_/gzhan yang sngon dang tha ma yi/_/de nyid sbyor ba'i cho ga can/_/bya ba'i rgyud rnams thams cad du/_/ji srid me yis 'jig gi bar/_/de der 'gro ba nyon mongs 'gyur/_/thams cad du ni sbas pa ste/_/
[\[44b.7\]]brtson 'grus pha rol 'joms pa'o/_/rdo rje sems dpa' mnyam ldan pa'i/_/rdo rje slob dpon bdag nyid che/_/myur du dngos grub skye 'gyur ba/_/'phral du yang dag yid ches byed/_/sgrub pos phyag rgya'i sbyor ba yis/_/nyon mi mongs par 'grub par 'gyur/_/dam tshig 
[\[45a\]]
[\[45a.1\]]mi ldan mi dag la/_/klog gam gal te byin gyur na/_/ngan pa'i bdag nyid bcom nas ni/_/ngan 'gro rnams su ltung bar 'gyur/_/g.yon gyi rgyud ni dmigs nas su/_/skye bo g.yon gyi spyod pa bya/_/thams cad g.yon pas byas na ni/_/myur du dngos grub 
[\[45a.2\]]'grub 'gyur zhes/_/sgyu ma kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las/_/dpa' bo thams cad dang mnyam par sbyor ba'i 
[\[45a.3\]]'khor lo 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i rim par phye ba ste bcu drug pa'o//_//de nas yang dag bshad bya ba/_/snying po thams cad bsdus pa ste/_/sems can kun la phan bya'i phyir/_/sgrub po'i bdud rtsi sbyor ba'i mchog_/ga bur dang ni gur kum dang /_/gan+d+ha gan+d+ha pa tra ste/_/
[\[45a.4\]]shing tsha dang ni sug smel bcas/_/ku sum dang ni de bzhin chu/_/pi si ta dang kun du ru/_/de bzhin ri yi zhag dang ni/_/ri skyes dang ni sbu gu'i zhag_/dri yi mar khu so dang ni/_/de bzhin lag skyes kyi ni rdul/_/gangs kyi chu dang snying po dang /_/rgya mtsho las skyes de dag ni/_/
[\[45a.5\]]lus can rnams la phan byed pa/_/gang gang la ni gangs chu bzhin/_/de ltar [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad rdzas rnams ni/_/bcud kyis len mchog phan byed yin/_/de nas brtag pa'i las dang ni/_/yon tan yan lag cho ga bshad/_/tsam pa ka dang ba ku la/_/nA ga ge sar ku mu ta/_/
[\[45a.6\]]ut+pal shi phA li ka dang /_/sbrang rtsi 'dzag dang pri yang ku/_/ku su ma ni nyid dag go/_/shrI ba la dang po so 'cha'/_/ka pi ta dang la ku tsha/_/ku ba dang ni brta ki/_/a ru ra dang ba ru ra/_/ba la ta ka skyu ru ra/_/e la rtsi dang pu na ka/_/e raN+Da dang ma ri tsi/_/de bzhin 
[\[45a.7\]]du ni pi pi ling /_/bca' ba dang ni shu dag dang /_/yung ba dang ni ru rta dang /_/bca' sga dang ni sgog skya dang /_/rlan can gyi ni do ba dang /_/nA ga pha la dkar po dang /_/de bzhin rgan po gzhon nur byed/_/glang chen dgra dang ri mdzes dang /_/'dzag dang byis pa stug po dang /_/
[\[45b\]]
[\[45b.1\]]ral pa dmar po smyo byed dang /_/mtshan mo'i shing dang myur 'gro dang /_/ka dam+ba dang gser shing ste/_/de bzhin du ni shi ri sha/_/ka ran dza dang shri kru dang /_/nim pa dang ni paM la si/_/de bzhin ga du ma dza ri/_/pa la sha dang tsan+dan gnyis/_/de bzhin a gar nag po 
[\[45b.2\]]dang /_/gur kum gur shing bye ka ra/_/sbrang rtsi mo k+Sha puSha ka ra/_/me tog 'bras bu rtsa ba dang /_/shun pa shing dang ro nyid do/_/khu byug zangs kyi gtsug phud can//bya rog dang ni gar ra dang /_/rma bya thi bya phug ron dang /_/drang srong bu stobs 'phel bar byed/_/ra dang 
[\[45b.3\]]ri bong [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]dang /_/sa li ga dang phag dang ni/_/khyad par du ni byi ba rnams/_/nad rnams thams cad 'joms par byed/_/me tog 'bras bu shun pa'i chang /_/de bzhin du ni 'bru yi chang /_/gzhan yang chang gi bye brag dag_/mdor bsdus nas ni btung ba'i mchog_/'di 
[\[45b.4\]]dag skyes pa bud med kyi/_/stobs rnams skyed pa'i sman yin te/_/nad rnams thams cad 'joms par byed/_/bza' dang btung dang bzhib pa dang /_/ldag pa dang ni mur ba dang /_/sna sman mgo bor bsku ba dang /_/bkru ba dang ni dril phyi dang /_/bza' dang btung sogs 
[\[45b.5\]]de dag ni/_/rnal 'byor pa yi sbyor ba mchog_/de ltar drug cu rtsa bzhi yi/_/rdzas dang [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad dag tu sbyar/_/sbom po phra mo'i sbyor ba rnams/_/skyes pa dang ni bud med kyang /_/chung ngu la sogs dbye ba ste/_/rlung dang mkhris pa bad kan sogs/_/re re zhing yang 
[\[45b.6\]]gsum gsum 'dod/_/nag po dkar po sngo bsangs te/_/'dzag dang de bzhin 'pho ba la'o/_/dgra dang mdza' bshes bar ma ste/_/khams kyi sde tshan bsdus pa yin/_/dpa' bo gcig dang skye gnyis rtsa/_/'bras bu chen po bska ba gsum/_/lhang tsher dang ni dngul

[\[46a\]]
[\[46a.1\]]chu'i 'bras bu ste/_/nyi ma'i 'od zer bsros nas ni/_/sbrang rtsi yi ni thigs pa blugs/_/thal sman lan tshwas bu gar bsgrub/_/ro ni bska ba rnams dang yang /_/lhang tsher dkar po'i skyo mo sbyin/_/dngul chu drug char bya ba mchog_/skyed byed don ni rga 
[\[46a.2\]]ba 'joms/_/cha ni tshad ldan 'ching byed de/_/'bras bus bya ba byed par 'gyur/_/sngon du gser shog blugs pa yis/_/rang skyon 'joms pa'i cho ga yin/_/de nas chu dang lan tshwa dang /_/thal ba'am rdul ni blug par bya/_/dron mo blugs na skra dkar skye/_/glang 
[\[46a.3\]]chen mgo yi bcud blangs la/_/seng ge'i tshad du btso bar bya/_/de nas slar ni phyung nas su/_/ar ka'i shing gi khu ba blugs/_/chos pa'i 'od kyis snying mig 'joms/_/tsan+d+ra dang ni ra sa na/_/sgog skya dang ni ker sa dang /_/a tra pa ti'i rus pa dang /_/de bzhin

[\[64a.4\]]rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las thams cad rgyu ba'i rim par phye ba ste nyi shu rtsa lnga pa'o//_//de nas bcom ldan 'das la lha mos mchod cing phyag byas nas 'di skad ces gsol to/_/sgang gshong 'khor lo khyod bshad pa/_/de rnams dbye

[\[64a.6\]]thams cad gnas/_/dngos po kun la khyab pa ste/_/lus la gnas kyang lus mi skye/_/dus nyid dus ni med pa dang /_/'jig rten nyid ni 'jig rten min/_/bden pa dang ni [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]pa min/_/lha khyod kyis ni sngar bshad pa'i/_/bsam gyis mi khyab dus mtshan nyid/_/gang

[\[64b.1\]]dang bral ba dang /_/khams ni snyoms par gnas pa dang /_/ran par za zhing tshul bzhin rgyu/_/de yi mig 'dzums gzung bar bya/_/mig 'dzum mig ni btsums pa ste/_/mig 'dzums drug la cha shas ni/_/gcig ces bya bar brjod pa yin/_/yud tsam drug ni skad

[\[65a\]]
[\[65a.1\]]dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las dus 'byung ba'i rim par phye ba ste nyi shu rtsa drug pa'o//_//de nas skar ma'i rigs gang yin/_/mdor bsdus nas ni bshad

[\[65b\]]
[\[65b.1\]]brjod/_/sdig pa bum pa seng ge dang /_/khyu mchog dag ni brtan pa ste/_/nya dang gnyis sbyor gzhu dang ni/_/bu mo rnams ni 'dren ma'o/_/lha yi bla ma nyi ma dang /_/mig dmar skyes pa'i gzugs yin te/_/pa ba sangs dang sgra gcan dang /_/zla ba mo yi gzugs
