[\[77b\]]
[\[77b.1\]]ka ni mchod byas la/_/de ltar byas nas lha mo che/_/gtor ma byin la yang yang bsrung /_/de yi ltag pa gshegs nas su/_/lce ni rtsa bar bcas te blangs/_/bring+gi rA dza gser shing dang /_/ka ga ga dza gzhan mi thub/_/lha yi sman dang bcas pa las/_/ril bu ru ni byas 
[\[77b.2\]]nas su/_/khar bcug nas ni bzung gyur na/_/mtshon cha thams cad zlog par 'gyur/_/ji snyed sa steng gnas pa yi/_/mtshon cha rnams kyang zlog byed na/_/kha cig la lta smos ci dgos/_/rims dang dug dang nad rnams dang /_/mkha' 'gro ma dang gdon gyis 'tshe/_/de dag 
[\[77b.3\]]thams cad zlog 'gyur te/_/gzhan dag tu ni nga mi smra/_/de nas sbyor ba gzhan bshad bya/_/de yi sha dang zhag dang khrag_/gcin dang khu bar bcas pa yis/_/yung ba nag po la byugs te/_/rgyal dang 'byung po'i nyi ma la/_/bgegs ni zhi bar bya ba'i phyir/_/'byung po kun gyi 
[\[77b.4\]]gtor byin te/_/las rnams thams cad bsgrub pa'i phyir/_/sngags pa cho gar ldan pa yis/_/byis pa'i ro yi lto bar ni/_/mar ngo 'byung bo'i nyi ma la/_/dur khrod kyi ni sa gzhi ru/_/cho ga bzhin du btsugs la bskyed/_/ce spyang la sogs bsrung bar bya/_/'dab ma brul 
[\[77b.5\]]zhing smin pa dang /_/sngar gyi cho ga bzhin blangs te/_/de ni lus la gnas kyi bar/_/skad cig gis ni dpa' bor 'gyur/_/sngar bstan byis pa'i lce dang ni/_/yung ba nag por bcas pa las/_/ri lur byas te khar bcug la/_/g.yul du zhugs na dgra bcings 'gyur/_/de 
[\[77b.6\]]nas dpung rnams thams cad ni/_/rmongs byed skud pa bshad par bya/_/skar ma rgyal la nan tan du/_/nags kyi ras bal blangs nas ni/_/zla mtshan gyis ni byugs byas la/_/sum sgril yang ni sum sgril du/_/gzhon nu ma yis 'bad de bkal/_/rkang pa brgyad pa'i 
[\[77b.7\]]sngags kyis ni/_/bzlas shing mdud pa de tsam bya/_/mdud pa dag ni thams cad kyang /_/ma mo brgyad du brtag par bya/_/de ltar sngags brgyad byas pa yis/_/byin gyis brlabs pa byas nas su/_/so so re re dag la yang /_/brgya rtsa brgyad brgyad bzlas byas la/_/lag 
[\[78a\]]
[\[78a.1\]]pa g.yon par btags na ni/_/mda' dang ral gri la sogs pa'i/_/mtshon cha thams cad bcings par 'gyur/_/sgyu ma chen po'i rig pa 'di/_/rab tu 'bad de gsang bar bya/_/de nas cho ga gzhan bshad pa/_/nags kyi shal ma li yi ni/_/bal ni 
[\[78a.2\]]skar ma rgyal la blangs/_/bu mo yi ni gci ba yis/_/btags te de dang bsre bar bya/_/bu mo yis ni bkal bar bya/_/gza' dang skar ma dge ba dang /_/zla ba stobs can dag la ni/_/mdud pa'i sbyor ba byas nas su/_/so so zhing ni re re la/_/sngar gyi rim pa'i sbyor ba yis/_/
[\[78a.3\]]shin tu mnyam par bzhag ste bsgrub/_/'di yis bcings pa tsam gyis ni/_/sum cu gsum pas kyang mi 'ching /_/las kyi cho ga mthong ba yis/_/mtshon cha thams cad 'ching 'gyur te/_/'di ni ba glang lhas su bsgrub/_/su rag ta dang seng ldeng dang /_/nim pa dang ni ar 
[\[78a.4\]]ka'ang rung /_/skar ma rgyal dang nyi ma la/_/shing bu de gnyis bzung nas ni/_/myos par byed pa'i rig pa che/_/rig pa kun gyi gtso mo ste/_/lan brgya ru ni bzlas nas su/_/shing bu phan tshun drud gyur na/_/dgra bo nang na 'thab par 'gyur/_/rdo rje yi ni chu dang ni/_/mdzub 
[\[78a.5\]]mo'i khrag dang bcas pa la/_/myos par byed ma'i rig pa bzla/_/sngags kyi bzlas pa sngon 'gro bas/_/dpral bar thig le byas na ni/_/chom po dang ni rkun po dang /_/'tshe bar byed pa gzhan yang rung /_/skye bo thams cad rmongs par byed/_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam 
[\[78a.6\]]sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las/_stobs skyed pa'i cho ga'i rim par phye ba ste sum cu rtsa dgu pa'o//_//gang gis 
[\[78a.7\]]sgrub pa po 'grub pa'i/_/de nas sbyor ba gzhan bshad bya/_/ras ster gyi ni sa bon dag_/snye ma'i rtser gnas blang bar bya/_/sngon bzhin [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]zla phyed kyi/_/'byung po'i nyi ma gza' bzang la/_/gau ri la sogs mkha' 'gro mar/_/bcas pa'i he ru ka mgon mchog_/
[\[78b\]]
[\[78b.1\]]bud med kyi ni me tog gis/_/yang yang du ni bsgo bar bya/_/bsgegs pa 'am ni mtshon gyis bsad/_/spyang ki la sogs ma bros pa'i/_/bud med thod pa mdzes pa ru/_/cho ga de yis bskyed par bya/_/de yang rnga mo dag gi ni/_/'khrul 'khor la ni bskyon nas su/_/de 
[\[78b.2\]]yi rtse mo blang bar bya/_/sngags pas mchod pa byas nas ni/_/bu mor bsgom zhing de bskul ba'i/_/chu ni 'bad dang bcas te gtor/_/bud med ro bsregs sa dang ni/_/thal bar bcas pas de bkang la/_/ras ster gyi ni sa bon nyid/_/thod pa der ni bskyed par bya/_/
[\[78b.3\]]bgegs dgra dang ni gshin gshed kyi/_/sngags ni mngon par bzlas pa yis/_/mchod dang gtor ma phul nas ni/_/ci rigs su ni bsrung bar bya/_/zhi ba'i tshigs bcad sngags kyis ni/_/brgya rtsa brgyad du bzlas pa'i chus/_/der ni bcu ste bskyed par bya/_/gzhan du yi 
[\[78b.4\]]dgas 'tshe ba 'byung /_/'bu la sogs pa bsrung ba dang /_/shing bskyed rig byed bzhin du bya/_/de ni 'bad de smin pa yi/_/ras bal dag ni bzung nas su/_/gzhon nu ma yi khrag gis ni/_/bsgos la sngar gyi bu mo yi/_/skra dang bcas pa bu mos bkal/_/skud pa de yis 
[\[78b.5\]]bcings gyur na/_/de ma thag tu skyes bu ni/_/'dod chags gdungs pa'i bud med 'gyur/_/bkrol na slar yang rnal mar 'gyur/_/bsgegs pa'i ro yi khrag dang ni/_/zhag gi yungs kar yang yang bsgo/_/gsang ba yi ni zhing sa ru/_/tshes brgyad skar ma rgyal la ni/_/
[\[78b.6\]]lam dang rigs mchog thod pa ni/_/ro bsregs sas bkang ro dang bcas/_/nyi ma shis pa dag la gzugs/_/de ni shin tu smin pa blang /_/ci rigs 'khrul 'khor btsir ba'i mar/_bskrad pa la sogs bya ba kun/_/blo ldan ji ltar 'dod bzhin byed/_/sa mtha' lnga 
[\[78b.7\]]ba yang dag ldan/_/rlung gi sa bon las byung ba/_/de yi mod la bskrad 'gyur te/_/bsgrub bya de ni slar mi ldog_/bsgegs nas bsad pa'i skyes bu'i ro/_/cho ga'i rim bzhin mchod byas la/_/sngar ni ji ltar bstan pa bzhin/_/mar des yan lag kun tu byug_/he ru 
[\[79a\]]
[\[79a.1\]]ka yi spyod pa yis/_/sngags ni zlos shing kun tu gnas/_/sbyor chen de ni bgegs bskrad bya/_/sngags pa chen pos de dus su/_/bgegs ni rnam pa sna tshogs pa/_/ro yi lus las byung mthong ste/_/de nas der ni de dus su/_/grub pa'i rdzas 
[\[79a.2\]]kyi bar chad byed/_/g.yog dang bcas pa'i sgrub pa pa pos/_/de tshe phyag rgya bcing bya ste/_/skyes gnas phyag rgya dpral ba yi/_/steng du bcings la bstan bya zhing /_/drag po yi ni gzugs kyis su/_/dpa' bo'i dam tshig ci rigs bstan/_/sha dang khrag tu bcas pa 
[\[79a.3\]]yi/_/gtor ma [{]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]rnams la sbyin/_/de yi lus las 'dzag pa'i mar/_/blangs te ku ba la sogs blugs/_/de yis lus la byugs tsam gyis/_/nam mkhar rgyu ba 'grub par 'gyur/_/gzhan yang ral gri mig sman dang /_/rtsi la sogs pa rab tu 
[\[79a.4\]]'grub/_/khrag dang mi yi zhag gis ni/_/yungs kar la ni bsgos nas su/_/mi ro yi ni khar bcug la/_/srung ba la sogs 'bad de bya/_/de nas snga ma bzhin bskyed pa'i/_/yungs kar gang la bsnun pa yi/_/bsgrub bya de ni rims nad kyis/_/'dzin te de ma thag 
[\[79a.5\]]tu 'chi/_/so bya yi ni khrag dang ni/_/zhag gis snum ster bsgo byas la/_/de yi thod pa ro bsregs pas/_/bkang ba ru ni bskyed par bya/_/mchod dang gtor ma la sogs pa/_/sngar bstan bzhin du ci rigs bya/_/smin pa'i 'bras bu khar bcug na/_/skyes bu bya 
[\[79a.6\]]yi gzugs su 'gyur/_/de ltar sa bon gzhan dag kyang /_/dog pa la gnas 'bras bu gzung /_/de dang 'dra ba'i gzugs 'gyur te/_/kha las bton na sngon bzhin 'gyur/_/bya rnams kyi ni tshe dang mtshungs/_/glang po'i gzugs sogs de bzhin bya/_/bi da ri 
[\[79a.7\]]yi sa bon ni/_/mar ngo skar ma rgyal dang ni/_/gza' ni nyi ma dag la gzung /_/ma du lung ga'i yar ngo la/_/mi ro'i zhag gis byugs nas su/_/zla ba gzas ni zin pa'i tshe/_/de yi yan lag mchog tu gzugs/_/zin pa de yi gzugs su 'gyur/_/de yi rus 
[\[79b\]]
[\[79b.1\]]pa rkang dang ni/_/sha la sogs pa bskyed par bya/_/de yi pags pa rus pa yi/_/phyag rgyas yan lag brgyan byas la/_/blo dang ldan pas sngar bstan bzhin/_/mchod pa byas te thal ba gtor/_/sngags dang bcas pa'i chu yis ni/_/mkhas pa yis ni rtag tu bcug_/
[\[79b.2\]]bgegs mthar byed dang gshin gshed kyi/_/sngags ni sngags pas rtag tu bzla/_/sha za la ni gtor ma sbyin/_/gzhan du na ni bton te khyer/_/sa bon de ni smin par gzung /_/lcug ma gsum gyis dkris nas su/_/khar bcug na ni sbed 'gyur te/_/nyi ma zla ba sogs pas 
[\[79b.3\]]kyang /_/'jig rten gsum 'dir mthong mi 'gyur/_/ma du lung ga gcig pu ni/_/khar bcug ma du lung gar 'gyur/_/bton na slar yang snang 'gyur te/_/'bras bskyed thun mong cho ga'o/_/de nas sngags kyi cho ga bshad/_/spyan ma'i sngags kyis gzugs par bya/_/mA ma kI yi 
[\[79b.4\]]sngags kyis bcu/_/sgrol ma'i sngags kyis bskyed par bya/_/de bzhin gos dkar mas ni bsrung /_/'di dag sa la sogs pa ste/_/thams cad 'dzin cing gso ba yin/_/des na 'bad pa thams cad kyis/_/spyan la sogs pas bsrung bar bya/_/de nas dgongs mo'i dus su 
[\[79b.5\]]ni/_/ku ba khan de'i sa bon dag_/mi yi zhag sogs kyis byugs la/_/bzhi mdo'i sas bkang thod par bskyed/_/de yi dpral bar thig le bya/_/bza' dang bca' sogs rnam pa lnga/_/mthong ba tsam gyis kha bar 'gyur/_/de nas thig le phyis na ni/_/de yi mod la 
[\[79b.6\]]rnal mar 'gyur/_/'ol ma se yi sa bon ni/_/bram ze yi ni thod pa ru/_/skar ma rgyal la sngags pas bskyed/_/de nas phyung ste slar yang ni/_/tha mal skyes bu'i thod par bskyed/_/sa bon de ni grub gyur na/_/kha ru bcug pas de gzugs 'gyur/_/skyes pa bud med ma 
[\[79b.7\]]ning sogs/_/bya yi cho ga bzhin du 'gyur/_/kha las bton na slar yang ni/_/tha mal gzugs 'gyur the tshom med/_/sbyor ba 'di ni grub pa ste/_/gzhan dag tu ni nga mi smra/_/sngags pas bung ba'i rgyal po'i sman/_/gsal shing bsad pa'i thod par bskyed/_/zhag 
[\[80a\]]
[\[80a.1\]]dang khrag gis gtor bar bya/_/de yi rtsa ba blangs nas su/_/srin lag gi ni khrag dang ni/_/zhag tu bcas pa bsre bar bya/_/spyi bor bzhag na rgyal bar 'gyur/_/lha la sogs pa dbang du byed/_/ba glang rna ba'i lcug ma ni/_/rnyed nas de yi sa bon ni/_/
[\[80a.2\]]smin pa bzung ste khar bcug nas/_/g.yul du zhugs na rgyal bar 'gyur/_/bring+ga rA dza bzung nas su/_/shan pa'i bu mo sngon mo yi/_/khrag gis zla ba mar ngo la/_/yang yang byugs te sbyar bar bya/_/ba glang rna ba'i lcug dang ni/_/skyes pa'i thod par bskyed dang bcas/_/
[\[80a.3\]]'bras bu chen po ster bar 'gyur/_/de la sogs pa'i rtsa ba dang /_/'bras bu tshul bzhin bskyed pa yis/_/bgegs rnams thams cad rlag byed par/_/sangs rgyas nges pa'i rgyud las gsungs/_/bsgrub par mi bya ba dang ni/_/'grub par mi 'gyur ci yang med/_/
[\[80a.4\]]man ngag dag ni ma gtogs par/_/dngos grub snang bar mi 'gyur ro/_/ko ka na ta'i 'bras bu ni/_/sna chen gyi ni thod par bskyed/_/dur khrod skyes pa'i bring+ga ni/_/thod par bskyed pa'i rtsa dang bcas/_/yar ngo mar ngo'i bcu bzhi la/_/bdud rtsi lnga dang lhan cig btags/_/
[\[80a.5\]]ril bu mdzes pa byas nas ni/_/grib ma la ni bskam par bya/_/de yis thig le byas nas ni/_/'gro kun bran bzhin dbang du 'gyur/_/am bra'i shing gi rus pa ni/_/mar khu chen pos byugs pa dang /_/de bzhin pa yi rnam lngas byug_/lag par khu ba byugs nas su/_/
[\[80a.6\]]'bras de gang la byin pa ni/_/de dbang 'gyur te gzhan du min/_/bal po se'u'i 'bras bu ni/_/khrag gis byugs te bud med kyi/_/thod par btsugs pa smin pa yi/_/'bras bu de ni mi dbang la/_/rgyal la byin na rtag dbang 'gyur/_/ku ba khan de'i sa bon ni/_/
[\[80a.7\]]nim ba la sogs 'bras bus byug_/ro bsregs sa dang ldan pa yi/_/chags can kham phor sa bon bskyed/_/shin tu mnyam par bzhag ste bsrung /_/mar ngo yi ni nyi ma la/_/nyin phyed nyi ma cha dus bsgrub/_/de ni blugs pa tsam gyis su/_/chang sogs 
[\[80b\]]
[\[80b.1\]]brlag 'gyur the tshom med/_/de nas gzhan yang bshad bya ba/_/yungs kar smin pa brgya dang ni/_/rtsa brgyad blangs nas zhag gis ni/_/bsgos te legs par thum por bya/_/bud med ro yi lto bar gzhug_/nyin bdun bzhag ste phyung nas su/_/de yi mchin 
[\[80b.2\]]par bcas pa'i sas/_/de yi thod pa bkang bar bskyed/_/gtor ma dang ni srung ba dang /_/bdug pa dang ni chus bcu sogs/_/shin tu 'bad dang bcas pa yis/_/las rnams thams cad sngon bzhin bya/_/ji srid smin gyi bar du bskyed/_/de nas sngar bzhin 
[\[80b.3\]]'bad pas blangs/_/bud med kyi ni khrag gis byug_/'dab ma dang ni sa bon sogs/_/de yi yan lag ci yang rung /_/bud med gang dang gang bsnun pa/_/de ni phyi bzhin 'brang bar 'gyur/_/'ol se'i sa bon 'bad de gzung /_/dmar ser thod par bskyed par bya/_/de 
[\[80b.4\]]yi lcug ma bzung nas su/_/blo dang ldan pas sgo nga bya/_/de yi nang du chang blugs na/_/snod der gnas pa'i rdzas rnams ni/_/ji srid ma zad bar du ni/_/de na gnas pa'ang zad mi 'gyur/_/chu 'gram sa dang rdza mkhan sa/_/de bzhin thong bar chags pa'i sa/_/grog mkhar 
[\[80b.5\]]gyi ni sa dag dang /_/mtshan ma gcig pa'i sa rnams ni/_/bskyed pa kun la shin tu bsngags/_/gci ba'i shul gyi sa bzung la/_/sngags kyis mngon par byin gyis brlab/_/bong bu'i gcin dang bcas pa las/_/mkhas pas mi yi gzugs brnyan bya/_/gser shing sngar 
[\[80b.6\]]bzhin sbyang byas la/_/sngags pas de yi khar bskyed bya/_/de yi yan lag lnga bzung ste/_/ril bu dag ni mdzes par bya/_/las rnams thams cad byed 'gyur te/_/khyad par du ni dbang du byed/_/yungs kar la sogs 'bras bu ni/_/glang po che yi sha dang ni/_/khrag 
[\[80b.7\]]dang rus pa'i zhag la sogs/_/de yi thal ba sogs pas byug_/de yi thod par bskyed pa dag_/de ni kha ru bcug tsam gyis/_/glang po che yi gzugs su 'gyur/_/'di ni thun mong cho ga ste/_/de bzhin rta dang ma he sogs/_/de bzhin stag dang ri bong sogs/_/cho ga de 
[\[81a\]]
[\[81a.1\]]bzhin bskyed pa yis/_/khrag bcas bsgoms nas stag sogs 'gyur/_/sbyor ba'i cho ga'i dus su ni/_/grub pa'i gza' la sogs dang sbyar/_/rtag tu sgom dang bzlas la dga'/_/dam tshig spyod pa bsrung ba dag_/de yis rtag tu 'grub 'gyur 
[\[81a.2\]]te/_/gzhan du na ni 'bras bu med/_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las 'bras bu bcos ma'i phyag rgya'i ye shes kyi rim par phye ba ste bzhi bcu pa'o//_//de nas A li 
[\[81a.3\]]kA li gnyis/_/de nyid las ni kun skyed cing /_/spro zhing sdud par byed pa po/_/tha snyad kyi ni cho ga ste/_/sngags kyi mtshan nyid thun mong ba/_/cho ga 'di dag ji bzhin nyon/_/a zhes bya ba mdog dmar ba/_/mgo dang thig le spangs pa ni/_/bsgrub bya'i snying ga mgo bo 
[\[81a.4\]]dang /_/gsang ba dang ni lte bar bsam/_A zhes bya ba thig ler ldan/_/mtshal dang 'ol ma se dang ni/_/ku su ma ni lta bu'i mdog_/bsgrub bya'i mgor ni bsam byas la/_/myos shing sems dang bral ba dang /_/lkugs shing rnam par nyams par bsam/_/
[\[81a.5\]]de yi lus ni rang nyid kyi/_/sa bon gyis dkrugs rmongs bsgoms na/_/ring por mi thogs dbang 'gyur te/_/rtag tu thams cad ster bar 'gyur/_/i zhes bya ba skas lta bu/_/dbyangs yig gsum pa'i thung ngu ni/_/bsgrub bya'i snying ga lte ba dang /_/de bzhin du ni 
[\[81a.6\]]mgrin par bsgom/_/I zhes bya ba'i dbyangs yig bzhi/_/li khri yi ni mdog lta bu/_/bsgrub bya yi ni dpral bar dgod/_/gang gi lus la gsum pa dang /_/bzhi pa'i mchog ni bkod pa yis/_/myos shing cha lugs nyams pa dang /_/sems dang bral bar 
[\[81a.7\]]byas nas su/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/lkugs pa bzhin du dbang du 'gyur/_/de nas dbyangs yig lnga pa ni/_/dbang po'i mtshon cha lta bu'i 'od/_/u zhes bya ba'i bsgrub bya yi/_/mgrin par zhags pa lta bur bsam/_/glog dang mtshungs par 'bar ba 
[\[81b\]]
[\[81b.1\]]yi/_/dbyangs yig mchog ni drug pa ste/_/U zhes bya ba bsgrub bya yi/_/snying gi dbus su bsgom par bya/_/lkugs shing myos par gyur pa dang /_/lus ni g.yo zhing sems dang bral/_/gang du sgrub pa por gnas par/_/'khrugs pa bzhin du 'ong bar 'gyur/_/
[\[81b.2\]]bdag nyid dang ni nor dang bcas/_/dbang du yang ni 'gyur ba yin/_/r-i zhes bya ba du ba'i mdog_/dpral ba lus dang bral ba ste/_/dbyangs yig gi ni dam pa de/_/snying ga dang ni gsang bar bsgom/_/de nas r-I ni mun pa'i mdog_/bsgrub bya'i lus kyi mgo bo dang /_/de 
[\[81b.3\]]bzhin rkang pa mgrin pa ru/_/ring po de ni bsgom par bya/_/bdun pa dang ni brgyad pa 'dis/_/myur du skrod par byed pa ste/_/sems ni rmugs shing nyams gyur nas/_/sa gzhi ma lus rgyu 'gyur te/_/mthong ba yi ni tshe 'di la/_/slar yang zlog par mi 'gyur ro/_/l-i 
[\[81b.4\]]zhes bya ba srid pa sdom/_/bya rog gi ni gshog pa 'dra/_/sgrub pos bsgrub bya'i mig dang ni/_/mgrin pa dang ni gsang bar bsgom/_/dbyangs yig ring po l-I zhes pas/_/bsgrub par bya ba dkris par bsam/_/bsgrub bya'i lus la bsgoms tsam gyis/_/sdang bar 
[\[81b.5\]]byed pa'i mchog yin te/_/sems can kun gyis smad pa dang /_/skye bo kun gyis brnyas pa dang /_/mar khu phyung ba'i 'ba' cha bzhin/_/skye bo kun gyis spong bar 'gyur/_/cog la ma yi mtshal dang ni/_/lham bu chu gsher lta bu'i mdog_/e zhes bya ba dbyangs kyi 
[\[81b.6\]]rgyal/_/gsang dang lte ba snying gar dgod/_/ai zhes bya ba gzi brjid che/_/li khri dang ni nyi ma'i mdog_/bsgrub bya'i mig dang de bzhin du/_/kha dang mgrin par bsgom par bya/_/sgrub pa'i mchog gis yid la ni/_/las 'di byas pa tsam gyis su/_/khams 
[\[81b.7\]]gsum po ni ma lus pa/_/mthe bo'i khyon tsam du ni mthong /_/gdong dang gdong ni mngon phyogs te/_/de yi yi ge'i cha shas kyis/_/dbugs kyi ri mo lcug mas dgug_/cha shas lcug ma'i dam tshig dbang /_/a zhes bya ba'i dbyangs yig ni/_/thig le dang ni zla phyed 
[\[82a\]]
[\[82a.1\]]bcas/_/zla ba gnyis ni tsheg drag bcas/_/'di ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi/_/yi ge mchog gi phyag rgya ste/_/sangs rgyas ri mor yang dag gnas/_/'di ltar A li kA li zhes/_/bcud len thams cad ces byar brjod/_/gtso bo la ni thim byas 
[\[82a.2\]]nas/_/rdo rje sems dpa' sogs dang sbyar/_/de yi 'og tu phyi nas ni/_/yi ge thams cad gzhug par bya/_/ji srid snang ba yod kyi bar/_/'dis ni bsrung ba byed par brjod/_/thig le zhes bya sems yin te/_/sems kyi rang bzhin stong pa yin/_/zla ba gnyis dang 
[\[82a.3\]]yang dag ldan/_/mi 'gyur mchog gi phyag rgya'o/_/de las dbyangs yig rnams kyi ni/_/sgrub po bdag nyid chen po yi/_/las ni lha dang lha min dang /_/mir bcas myur du dbang du byed/_/oM zhes bya ba nyi ma ltar/_/'od zer 'bar ba stong dang ldan/_/bsgrub bya yi 
[\[82a.4\]]ni mgo bo dang /_/dpral ba khyad par snying gar bsgom/_/oM zhes bya ba rtag 'bar ba/_/nyi ma bye ba'i gzi brjid can/_/bsgrub bya yi ni lus kun la/_/khyab pa nyid du bsgom par bya/_/bskal pa yi ni me dpung ltar/_/dbyangs kyis khyab nas bsgom pa dang /_/
[\[82a.5\]]dbyangs kyi me yis bsgrub bya yang /_/rab tu 'bar bar bsgoms byas nas/_/dgra bo lo brgyar 'tsho ba yang /_/de ma thag tu ltung bar 'gyur/_/a ni thams cad sdud pa ste/_/'tshe ba zlog par byed pa'ang yin/_/A ni gshegs su gsol ba ste/_/ji ltar 
[\[82a.6\]]'dod bzhin lha yang byed/_/bud med kun gyi mngal du ni/_/bsam na khu ba 'dzag par 'gyur/_/khyad par du ni gzhan dag la'ang /_/dkar po dmar po'i ting 'dzin bya/_/a la sogs dang yi ge rnams/_/lus la bkod na dpyad mi dgos/_/de ltar dbyangs 
[\[82a.7\]]kyi las rnams ni/_/khyod la bdag gis gang bstan la/_/dbyangs yig dge ba bcu drug ni/_/lus la go char bkod de bya/_/A zhes bya ba rkang gnyis dgod/_/a ni byin pa gnyis la bsam/_/I ni brla la dgod bya ste/_/i zhes bya ba gsang bar gnas/_/u 
[\[82b\]]
[\[82b.1\]]ni lte ba'i rtsa ba ste/_/U zhes bya ba lte ba'i dbus/_/r-i ni lte ba'i steng du dgod/_/r-I zhes bya ba snying gar yin/_/l-i zhes bya ba mgrin par dgod/_/de bzhin l-I ni rkan la'o/_/e zhes bya ba kha la ste/_/de bzhin e ni sna la'o/_/o ni rna bdag la ste/_/au zhes bya ba dpral 
[\[82b.2\]]bar ro/_/oM ni mgo la dgod byas te/_/aHzhes bya ba rgyab tu dgod/_/dbang dang dgug la dmar por te/_/gsad la thams cad sngon por ro/_/du ba lta bur bsams na ni/_/bskrad pa nyid 'gyur the tshom med/_/dbyangs yig kun gyis byin brlabs 'di/_/bdag 
[\[82b.3\]]gi de nyid gsal ba yin/_/sgrub po bdag nyid dam pa yis/_/de ltar go cha bgos nas ni/_/sgrub pos dbyangs dang yi ge yi/_/phyi nas las rnams shin tu sbyar/_/de nas rig pa'i de nyid 'byung /_/las rnams rtag tu byas pa ste/_/ka nas ma yi mthar thug pa'i/_/
[\[82b.4\]]las ni blo gros chen pos nyon/_/lha dang lha min mi dang bcas/_/gang gis sa gsum thams cad ni/_/sgrub po shin tu rgyas pa yi/_/dbang du de dag thams cad 'gyur/_/yid la 'dod pa'i 'bras ster ba/_/de ni nga yis bshad par bya/_/dbyangs yig bcu drug po rnams 
[\[82b.5\]]kyi/_/mdzes ma khyod la sngar bshad nas/_/da ni ka sogs yi ge yi/_/las rnams 'dod bzhin byed pa bshad/_/lha mo yi ge dang po ni/_/nyi ma'i gzi brjid dang mtshungs shing /_/li khri spungs pa lta bu yi/_/thig ler ldan pa'i yi ge che/_/sgrub pos bsgrub bya'i snying ga 
[\[82b.6\]]dang /_/lte ba dang ni mgo bor bsgom/_/de yis bsgrub bya'i lus kyi ni/_/nang du phug ste khyab par bsam/_/de yis lus ni 'khrugs nas su/_/rmongs shing sems dang bral ba dang /_/brgyal bzhin du ni dbang du 'gyur/_/ji srid lus la gnas kyi bar/_/de srid 
[\[82b.7\]]dbang du 'gyur ba ste/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/bdag nyid nor dang bcas 'gyur la/_/kha zhes bya ba'ang brtson chen ma/_/lham bu chu gsher lta bu dang /_/nyi ma 'char kha lta bu'i mdog_/thig ler bcas pa gzi brjid che/_/gsal zhing 'od dang bcas pa ste/_/
[\[83a\]]
[\[83a.1\]]bsgrub bya yi ni snying ga dang /_/mgo dang rkang pa kun tu dgod/_/de yis bsgrub bya phug nas ni/_/rmongs shing sems dang bral bar bsam/_/sgrub pa po yi mchog gis ni/_/de yi lus ni dkrus pa dang /_/myos shing brgyal bar bsgom par bya/_/de yis 
[\[83a.2\]]shin tu rgyal 'gyur zhing /_/yi ge'i dbang pos che bas na/_/sgrub po bdag nyid chen po yi/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/nor dang bcas pa dbang du 'gyur/_/ga zhes bya ba'i sa bon che/_/rang gi lha yi sdom pa ste/_/tsheg drag bcas pa gzi brjid che/_/gtso bo char 
[\[83a.3\]]sprin lta bu'i mdog_/bsgrub bya'i mgo dang mgrin pa dang /_/khyad par du ni snying gar bsam/_/de yis bsgrub par bya ba'i lus/_/yi ges kun tu phug bsam na/_/lus ni rtag tu dkrugs pas na/_/'dar zhing de yi dbang du 'gyur/_/bdag nyid dang ni nor dang 
[\[83a.4\]]bcas/_/sdang ba'i dgra rnams ma lus pa/_/khyim la sogs pa kun tu ni/_/ji srid nyi zla yod kyi bar/_/ma lus par ni 'joms par byed/_/g+ha zhes bya ba sgyu ma che/_/bskal pa'i me dang gzi brjid mtshungs/_/steng du thig les brgyan pa ste/_/ku su ma yi khu ba 
[\[83a.5\]]'dra/_/bsgrub bya yi ni lte ba dang /_/gsang ba snying ga kha dang ni/_/mgo bo ru ni bsams tsam gyis/_/bsgrub bya dga' bas myos nas su/_/sems dang bral zhing nyams nas ni/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/bdag nyid nor dang bcas pa ru/_/shin tu de yi dbang du 
[\[83a.6\]]'gyur/_/nga zhes bya ba gzi brjid che/_/sde tshan gyi ni mtha' mar gnas/_/de ni 'dod pa'i mda' yin te/_/'jig rten gsum po dbang du byed/_/de ni bsgrub bya'i gsang bar bsam/_/khu ba dag ni 'dzag par byed/_/gsang ba ru ni zhugs pa yis/_/thams cad 
[\[83a.7\]]du ni 'bigs par 'gyur/_/yan lag gi ni tshigs kun tu/_/de yis phug pas myos nas ni/_/nor dang bdag nyid bcas pa ni/_/myur ba nyid du dbang du 'gyur/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/de la chags dang bral mi 'gyur/_/yi ge lnga po kun gyis kyang /_/
[\[83b\]]
[\[83b.1\]]bdag nyid go cha bskon par bya/_/ka zhes bya ba snying gar dgod/_/kha zhes bya ba mgo la ste/_/ga zhes bya ba spyi gtsug tu/_/g+ha zhes bya ba go char bya/_/nga zhes bya ba sgrub pa po'i/_/lag pa g.yon la dgod par bya/_/de nas bsgrub bya dbang dang ni/_/dgug dang gsad la 
[\[83b.2\]]sogs par sbyar/_/cho ga de ltar byas na ni/_/dpyad mi dgos par dngos grub 'gyur/_/tsa zhes bya ba du ba'i mdog_/rlung gi gnas kyi yi ge ste/_/sgrub pos de yi snying ga dang /_/mgo dang rkang par bsam par bya/_/bsgrub bya rlung la gnas nas ni/_/kun tu 'pho bar 
[\[83b.3\]]bsam pa dang /_/sems dang bral bar gyur nas su/_/nam mkha' la ni gnas bzhin du/_/rlung gis gzhan dang gzhan dag tu/_/khyer ba ru ni bsgom par bya/_/bsam gtan sbyor ba 'di yis ni/_/bsgrub bya shin tu brtan pa yang /_/bskrad 'gyur 'dir ni the tshom med/_/
[\[83b.4\]]bsgrub bya'i lus ni 'phangs pas na/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/slar yang 'ong bar mi 'gyur ro/_/tsha zhes bya ba'i yi ge che/_/gsal zhing mig sman nag po'i mdog_/cha gnyis pa dang yang dag ldan/_/bsgrub bya yi ni mgo bor bsam/_/de yi mgo la du ba yi/_/
[\[83b.5\]]tshogs dang mtshungs pa'i rlung gis khyab/_/cung zad tsam gyis bsgrub bya yi/_/lus ni de yis bskor bar bsam/_/slar yang tsha ni tsheg drag bcas/_/lte ba ru ni bsams nas su/_/lan gsum du ni brjod byas na/_/yi ge 'di ni bskrad pa'i mchog_/bsgrub bya 
[\[83b.6\]]rmongs shing sems dang bral/_/dgra bo mdangs ni nyams nas su/_/sa stengs ma lus pa ru 'khyam/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/slar yang ldog par mi 'gyur ro/_/dza zhes bya ba'i yi ge rgyal/_/du ba'i mdog can gzi brjid che/_/'od zer ldan zhing shin tu 'bar/_/rdul 
[\[83b.7\]]phran dag ni stong du 'phro/_/bsgrub bya yi ni mgo bo dang /_/gsang bar yi ge zhugs par bsgom/_/yan lag tshigs kun phug pa dang /_/sems dang bral ba bzhin du gnas/_/yi ges yongs su bkang bas na/_/de nas brgyal bar gyur nas ni/_/nyin dang mtshan 
[\[84a\]]
[\[84a.1\]]du rmongs pa dang /_/mi sad par ni bsgom par bya/_/sa stengs shing dang nags tshal bcas/_/thams cad du ni 'gro bar 'gyur/_/bskrad pa'i cho ga 'di dag ni/_/dge ma khyod la nga yis bshad/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/slar yang 'ong bar mi 
[\[84a.2\]]'gyur ro/_/dz+ha zhes bya ba stobs po che/_/bsgrub bya yi ni snying ga ru/_/nyi ma lta bur 'bar ba dang /_/'od zer gsal ba rnam par 'phro/_/thig le zla ba phyed pas brgyan/_/bsgrub bya'i mgo dang snying ga dang /_/lte ba'i steng du yi ge bsam/_/sgrub pa'i dbang pos bsgrub 
[\[84a.3\]]bya'i lus/_/'bar bas bcud dang phral bar bsam/_/de yis bsgrub bya brgyal 'gyur zhing /_/bskrad pa'am ni 'chi ba'am/_/yang na smyon pa nyid du 'gyur/_/yi ge yis ni ltung bas na/_/slar yang 'ong bar mi 'gyur te/_/sa gzhi nags tshal bcas pa ru/_/
[\[84a.4\]]smyo zhing 'gro 'gyur the tshom med/_/dz+ha zhes bya ba 'di yis ni/_/myur du bskrad 'gyur the tshom med/_/nya zhes bya ba'i yi ge mchog_/sna tshogs pa yi mdog dang ldan/_/pa la sha yi me tog gi/_/'od zer stong phrag 'bar ba ste/_/chu rgyun stong ni 'bab pa yis/_/de yi 
[\[84a.5\]]lus la dbo bar bya/_/de yis nor dang bcas pa ru/_/brgyal zhing sems dang bral ba dang /_/rmongs shing myur du dbang du 'gyur/_/Ta zhes bya ba'i yi ge mchog_/dbang po'i mtshon cha lta bu'i mdog_/'od zer 'bar ba stong phrag gis/_/yi ge 'bar dang ldan par 
[\[84a.6\]]bsam/_/bsgrub bya yi ni dpral ba dang /_/mgrin pa dang ni snying gar bsam/_/bsgrub bya'i steng du nam mkha' la/_/bdud rtsi sgo yi thig le ni/_/spyi bo'i steng du rtag tu bsam/_/cung zad glog ni g.yo 'dra ba'i/_/kha ba dag ni 'bab par bsam/_/sgrub pa po 
[\[84a.7\]]yis bsgrub bya ni/_/yan lag tshigs kun g.yo ba dang /_/brgyal zhing rmongs la myos bsams na/_/ji srid nyi zla yod bar du/_/nor dang bdag nyid bcas pa ru/_/de yis thams cad dbang du 'gyur/_/de ltar Ta yi rgyal po yi/_/las ni khyod la nga yis bshad/_/
[\[84b\]]
[\[84b.1\]]de nas yi ge gnyis pa yi/_/las kyi mchog ni bshad par bya/_/Tha zhes bya ba'i yi ge ni/_/dbang chen dang ni 'dra bar 'dod/_/spyi bor bdud rtsi theg les brgyan/_/de bzhin glog dang 'brug gis brgyan/_/yi ge'i mchog ni bsgom par bya/_/bdud rtsi chu rgyun 'bab pa 
[\[84b.2\]]yis/_bsgrub bya'i lus la kun tu gtor/_/bsgrub bya'i mgo dang mgrin pa dang /_/snying ga ru ni gnas par bsam/_/bsgrub bya'i mgo ni phug pa dang /_/lus la kun tu khyab pa yis/_/bsgrub bya myos shig 'dar ba ru/_/sngags pas yi ges rtag tu bsgom/_/de 
[\[84b.3\]]nas ji srid 'tsho bar du/_/brgyal zhing rmongs pas dbang du 'gyur/_/de nas Da zhes bya ba ni/_/yi ge'i rgyal po gsum pa ste/_/de yi las kyi mchog rnams ni/_/rtse gcig yid kyis gus pas nyon/_/Da zhes bya ba thig ler bcas/_/rdo rje lta bu'i dbyibs can yin/_/ut+pa 
[\[84b.4\]]la ltar ljang ser te/_/'od zer gsal ba kun tu 'phro/_/bsgrub bya'i spyi bor bsam pa yis/_/bsgrub bya'i tshigs ni 'bugs shing 'byed/_/bsgrub bya mig yor bsgom byas na/_/lus ni kun tu g.yo bzhin du/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/rmongs dang bcas pas 
[\[84b.5\]]dbang du 'gyur/_/de nas las kyi mchog gzhan ni/_/shin tu gnyer bar byed pa ste/_/D+ha zhes bya ba yi ge'i rgyal/_/shin tu rmad byung byed pa po/_/dbang po'i mtshon cha lta bu'i mdog_/nyi ma lta bur 'bar ba ste/_/bsgrub bya'i snying dang lte ba dang /_/mgrin par yi ge 
[\[84b.6\]]bsams nas su/_/byang chub sems ni rab 'dzag pas/_/de yi lus la dbo bar bya/_/de yis bzi zhing brgyal nas su/_/gnyid log par ni bsgom byas na/_/'di yis ring por mi thogs par/_/rmongs shing sgrub po'i dbang du 'gyur/_/cho ga'i sbyor ba 'di yis ni/_/nam 
[\[84b.7\]]yang chags dang bral mi 'gyur/_/de nas d+ha mtha' yi ge'i las/_/ji ltar 'dod bzhin 'bad de nyon/_/Na zhes bya ba du ba'i mdog_/cung zad 'bar zhing gsal ba ste/_/dri ma med pa'i du ba las/_/'od zer gsal ba rnam par 'phro/_/bsgrub bya yi ni lus kun la/_/
[\[85a\]]
[\[85a.1\]]de yis rnam par khyab byed cing /_/'od zer glog stong 'khyug 'dra ba/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i steng du bsgom/_/'od zer brgya yis bkang bas na/_/myos nas su ni brgyal bar 'gyur/_/de yis ji srid 'tsho bar du/_/de ni de yi dbang du 'gyur/_/ta zhes 
[\[85a.2\]]bya ba'i yi ge mchog_/'od zer glog ltar gsal ba ste/_/mdog ni rma bya'i mgrin pa 'dra/_/yi ge 'di ni bsgrub bya yi/_/snying dang mgrin pa gsang ba dang /_/lte bar bkod de 'bugs par bsam/_/yi ge de yis rmongs pas na/_/bsgrub bya sems dang bral ba 
[\[85a.3\]]dang /_/rmongs shing brgyal bar gyur nas su/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/myos shing dbang 'gyur gzhan du min/_/de yi drung na gnas pa yi/_/tha zhes bya ba yi ge mchog_/mu men gyi ni mdog 'dra dang /_/ut+pal sngon po'i 'dab ma'i 'od/_/bsgrub bya'i snying ga 
[\[85a.4\]]dpral ba dang /_/de bzhin du ni rkang par dgod/_/bsgrub bya'i lus la dbang po yi/_mtshon cha bzhin du thim par bsam/_/yi ge'i rgyal pos mnan pas na/_/bsgrub bya sems dang bral 'gyur zhing /_/sa 'dzin gyi ni dbang bo bzhin/_/'gro ba dag kyang bcings par 
[\[85a.5\]]'gyur/_/reg bya mi shes dri mi shes/_/sgra mi thos shing gzugs mi mthong /_/lce yis ro rnams mi myong zhing /_/dug gis brgyal ba bzhin du gnas/_/gang dang gang gi steng du ni/_/nyi 'od lta bu'i yi ge bsam/_/yi ge'i rgyal po tha yis ni/_/skyes bu bcings 
[\[85a.6\]]'gyur the tshom med/_/de nas gzhan yang da yi ni/_/stong pa'i las kyi cho ga nyon/_/da zhes bya ba mdog sngon ni/_/char sprin nag po'i 'od dang mtshungs/_/'od zer 'bar ba rnam 'phro ba/_/bsgrub bya yi ni mgor bsam la/_/me stag 'phro ba bsgoms pa ni/_/phyogs 
[\[85a.7\]]kun tu ni bsam par bya/_/yi ge'i dbang phyug de yis ni/_/bsgrub bya'i mgo bo phug par bsam/_/'bar ba'i sprin ni stong phrag gis/_/de ni kun tu bskor bar bsam/_/de ni bkod pa tsam gyis su/_/sdong gog dang ni mtshungs par 'gyur/_/phug ste shin tu 
[\[85b\]]
[\[85b.1\]]bcings pas na/_/'gro ba'ang mi g.yo bar ni 'gyur/_/ji srid sgrub po 'dod bar du/_/sems dang bral zhing brtan par gnas/_/slar yang sgrub pos bsams tsam gyis/_/rmongs pa las ni grol bar 'gyur/_/de nas yi ge d+ha yi ni/_/las ni rmad du gyur pa nyon/_/b+ha zhes 
[\[85b.2\]]bya ba gzhu yi dbyibs/_/kha dog ser po gser gyi mdog_/gser lta bu yi 'od zer 'phro/_/bsgrub bya yi ni mgrin pa ru/_/zhags pa lta bur 'bar bar bsam/_/sgrub pa'i dbang pos bsgrub bya de/_/khrid de 'ong bar bsgom byas na/_/sgrub pa po ni gar gnas par/_/
[\[85b.3\]]rmongs dang bcas te 'ong bar 'gyur/_/ji srid 'tsho yi bar du ni/_/phyugs de sems dang bral 'gyur zhing /_/nyi ma brgyad pa'i cha la ni/_/rang gi gnas nas 'ong bar 'gyur/_/de nas yi ge na yi ni/_/las kyi cho ga'i sgrub pa nyon/_/mdog ni du ba'i phung po 'dra/_/rlung gi dkyil 
[\[85b.4\]]'khor 'khrugs dang ldan/_/bsgrub bya'i snying ga mgrin pa dang /_/lte ba dang ni rkang par sbyar/_/de yis dgra rnams rang yul nas/_/bskrad 'gyur 'dir ni the tshom med/_/de ni rmongs pas rang yul du/_/nam yang phyis ni 'ong mi 'gyur/_/de nas sde tshan lnga pa ni/_/yi ge 
[\[85b.5\]]rnams kyi dbang phyug mchog_/las ni shin tu grub pa ste/_/gzhan dang gzhan ni bshad par bya/_/pa zhes bya ba rlung gi gnas/_/char sprin gyi ni sprin gyis bskor/_/'bar zhing du bas 'khrigs par ldan/_/bsgrub bya yi ni mgo bo dang /_/mgrin pa snying ga rked pa dang /_/
[\[85b.6\]]rkang pa dag tu bsams nas ni/_/pa zhes bya ba'i yi ge des/_/bsgrub bya'i lus ni dkri bar bya/_/bsgrub par bya ba nga rgyal dang /_/khro bas brtan gnas gtso bo yang /_/yi ge gtso bo bkod pa yis/_/skad cig gis ni bskrad 'gyur te/_/gal te tshe rabs gzhan dag 
[\[85b.7\]]la'ang /_/slar zhing ldog par mi 'gyur ro/_/de nas yi ge pha yi ni/_/las mchog nga yis bshad kyis nyon/_/yi ge pad+ma'i mdog 'dra zhing /_/'bar ba bsgrub bya'i spyi bo dang /_/mgrin pa snying ga lte ba dang /_/gsang ba'i gnas su bkod nas ni/_/ku su ma yi khu 
[\[86a\]]
[\[86a.1\]]'dra ba/_/'dzag pa ru ni rnam bsams te/_/bsgrub bya'i lus ni phyi nang du/_/de yis gang bar bsgom byas na/_/bsgrub bya rmongs shing brgyal 'gyur la/_/sems med lus ni 'dar gyur nas/_/rmongs pas sgrub pa po yi ni/_/dbang du 'gyur te dpyad mi 
[\[86a.2\]]dgos/_/ba zhes bya ba'i rgyal po ni/_/gru bzhi pa la stobs po che/_/dung dang zla 'dra dkar po'i mdog_/zla ba'i 'od can bsgrub bya ni/_/dbus su sgrub pos par bsam/_/de lus dug gis mi tshugs shing /_/nad la sogs pas gzir mi 'gyur/_/rims dang 
[\[86a.3\]]bkres dang skom mi 'byung /_/'di ni rtag tu bsgoms gyur na/_/tshe ring nad med rgyal bar 'gyur/_/bsam gtan sbyor ba 'di yis ni/_/zla ba dmar dang bcas pa ru/_/lhan cig tu ni bzlas gyur na/_/rtag tu bdud rtsi'i rgyun 'drar 'gyur/_/de nas rgyal po b+ha zhes 
[\[86a.4\]]pa'i/_/las kyi mchog ni mnyan par gyis/_/dbang po'i mtshon cha lta bu ste/_/nyi ma 'char kha lta bu'i mdog_/bsgrub bya yi ni mgo bo ru/_/rgyal ba'i mchog ni rtag bsams la/_/de yi lus ni ma lus par/_/yi ge'i rgyal pos dbug par bya/_/rmongs shing 
[\[86a.5\]]sems dang bral 'gyur te/_/rdo rje yin yang brgya la gyur nas/_/mche bas zin ltar skad cig la/_/ltung zhing 'chi ba nyid gyur pas/_/de yi srog ni rmongs par 'gyur/_/dgra ni lo brgyar 'tsho ba yang /_/skad cig gis ni 'chi bar 'gyur/_/de nas gzi brjid chen po 
[\[86a.6\]]yi/_/yi ge nyi shu rtsa lnga pa/_/ma zhes bya ba dam pa mchog_/de yi las ni ji bzhin nyon/_/tsam pa ka ltar ser ba'i mdog_/bskal pa'i me bzhin gzi brjid 'bar/_/'od ni glog stong 'khyug pa bzhin/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i steng bsams la/_/de yi 'od kyis 
[\[86a.7\]]bsgrub bya yi/_/steng dang 'og ni ma lus par/_/lus ni kun tu g.yogs bsams na/_/gnod sbyin lha dang klu yi bu/_/sha za dang ni srin po dang /_/khams gsum yi ge'i bdag po dang /_/lha dang lha min mi dang bcas/_/sgrub po bdag nyid chen po yi/_/skad cig gis 
[\[86b\]]
[\[86b.1\]]ni dbang 'gyur bar/_/mdor bsdus ngas ni khyod la bshad/_/ya zhes bya ba yi ge mchog_/mun pa dang ni du ba'i mdog_/rlung gi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas shing /_/rlung gi gzugs can yi ge che/_/rlung bdun gyi ni gtso bo dang /_/gnas zhes bya bar brjod pa ste/_/zla 
[\[86b.2\]]phyed kyi ni dbyibs can la/_/ba dan brgya yis rnam par bklubs/_/rnam pa bzhi yi rten gyur pa/_/phyi rol gos ltar dkris pa ste/_/ra zhes bya ba yi ge mchog_/'bar ba bye ba stong dang ldan/_/de yi steng du bsam par bya/_/du ba yi ni phreng bas brgyan/_/bsgrub bya'i mgo 
[\[86b.3\]]bor bsgoms nas ni/_/dgra bo lo brgya 'tsho ba yang /_/sbrul gyi mche bas zin pa ltar/_/skad cig tsam gyis srog dang ni/_/bral zhing 'chi ba'i sgor gzhol 'gyur/_/lha sogs thams cad gang rung gi/_/steng du yi ge 'di sbyar na/_/myur du lhung 'gyur dpyad mi dgos/_/
[\[86b.4\]]gdon dang rims rnams thams cad ni/_/de la 'bab par 'gyur ba dang /_/sbrul chen brgya yi mche ba 'bab/_/de yis myur du 'chi 'gyur na/_/gnod pa gzhan lta smos ci dgos/_/ya zhes pa dang ra zhes pa/_/rlung dang me yi gzugs can du/_/nam mkha'i 
[\[86b.5\]]phyogs su bsams nas ni/_/nam mkha'i ngos ni g.yogs bsams na/_/nyi zla gza' skar sogs pa'ang rung /_/chu bo dang ni rgya mtsho yi/_/dbus su yi ge gnyis po dag_/bsams nas nyi ma brgyad pa la/_/rgya mtsho la sogs thams cad skam/_/zhing sa 'bru yi phung po dang /_/
[\[86b.6\]]khyim la sogs pa'i gnas bsam na/_/phyi rol me lce 'bar ba yis/_/thams cad yi ge thal bar byed/_/me yi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas te/_/rlung gis kun tu bskor bar ni/_/yi ge mchog ni gnyis bsams na/_/las ni kun tu nye bar gnas/_/de nas gzhan 
[\[86b.7\]]yang yi ge gnyis/_/stobs chen ldan pa bshad par bya/_/dbang chen dang ni de bzhin chu/_/las rnams thams cad byed pa po/_/la zhes bya ba sa yin te/_/sa zhes bya ba 'dzin pa'i mchog_/dbang po rdo rjes mtshan pa dang /_/'od zer glog ltar 'bar ba ste/_/kha 
[\[87a\]]
[\[87a.1\]]dog lnga dang ldan pa ru/_/yi ge'i dbang po sa la bsgom/_/de steng cu yi yi ge ni/_/bzhi pa legs par gnas pa ste/_/dung dang zla 'dra dkar po'i mdog_/kha ba'i rgyun ni 'bab pa ste/_/la yi steng du bkod nas su/_/shin tu bsil 
[\[87a.2\]]ba'i bdud rtsi 'dzag_/dbang chen gyis ni bskor ba yi/_/chu yi dkyil 'khor dbus gnas pa/_/spyi bor thig le dang ni ldan/_/char pa'i rgyun ni stong phrag 'bebs/_/steng du sprin gyi phung po ni/_/glog dang 'brug la sogs pas brgyan/_/bdud rtsi char gyi rgyun 
[\[87a.3\]]'bebs pa/_/nam mkha' thams cad khyab pa ru/_/dkyil 'khor gnyis kyi dbus 'gyur ba/_/gang gi mgo yi steng bsgoms na/_/sgrub po bdag nyid chen po yi/_/las ni ji ltar 'dod bzhin byed/_/gdengs ka gsum gyis mdzes pa yi/_/lhag ma can ni 
[\[87a.4\]]zhi gzugs bsam/_/de yi mig gnyis dbang po yi/_/mtshon chas brgyan pa'i dbus su ni/_/bdud rtsi 'bebs pa'i i yig dgod/_/grong khyer gyi ni mtha' ma dang /_/bsgrub bya yi ni steng du dbab/_/dug dang gdon dang rims la sogs/_/skad cig gis ni zhi bar 
[\[87a.5\]]'gyur/_/da ni skra dkar gzhom pa yi/_/cho ga bshad kyis mnyan par gyis/_/dkyil 'khor nyis 'gyur yi ge ni/_/de ltar rtag tu bsams gyur na/_/tshe thung ba yang ring 'gyur na/_/skra dkar la sogs smos ci dgos/_/bud med dbang phyug rnams la ni/_/'bad 
[\[87a.6\]]pas yang yang g.yon bskor zhing /_/rang gi spyi bo'i steng du ni/_/lo gcig bar du bsgom byas na/_/rga ba dang ni 'chi ba 'joms/_/nad kyis gdung bar mi 'gyur te/_/gal te klu chen brgyad kyi ni/_/mche ba'i dug gis zin gyur kyang /_/dug ni dug med byas nas su/_/
[\[87a.7\]]dug gis brgyal ba slong bar mdzad/_/'gron po lam gyis dub pa dang /_/gdon la sogs pas gzir ba rnams/_/kun las thar bar 'gyur ba yin/_/bdag gi spyi bor bsams tsam gyis/_/skad cig gis ni bkres skom 'joms/_/nad rnams kun gyis gdungs 
[\[87b\]]
[\[87b.1\]]pa la/_/yi ge bkod pas grol bar 'gyur/_/de ltar las rnams sna tshogs ni/_/lha mo khyod la bshad pa yin/_/gtso mo bzhi yi rnam pa bzhi/_/las kyi cho ga rgyas pa ste/_/rlung dang me dang bdud rtsi bcud/_/de bzhin drod kyi sde pa ste/_/gshin rje brtan ma phyag 
[\[87b.2\]]gnyis pa/_/rgyan rnams kun gyis rnam par bklubs/_/lus ni me las byung ba ru/_/bskal ba'i me lce lta bur bsam/_/'og dang steng du wa yis brgyan/_/de yi phyogs kun thams cad du/_/'bar ba gang gi snying gar bsam/_/'od zer 'bar ba rnam par 'phro/_/
[\[87b.3\]]wa dang ldan pa'i yi ge che/_/nam mkha' khyab pas bcings 'gyur te/_/brtan ma yi ni 'khrul 'khor gyi/_/yi ge'i rgyal pos bcings pas na/_/sa 'dzin lta bur brtan par 'gyur/_/rkang med dang ni rkang gnyis dang /_/rkang bzhi la sogs sems can gyi/_/
[\[87b.4\]]lte ba ru ni bsgom byas na/_/de ma thag tu 'chi bar 'gyur/_/de yang bskal pa'i me lta bu'i/_/'od zer 'bar ba 'phro ba yi/_/wa ni steng du bsgom par bya/_/slar yang me yis 'bar bsam na/_/gang gi lag pa g.yon pa la/_/yi ge de ni dgod par bsam/_/ji 
[\[87b.5\]]srid lag tu bsam gyi bar/_/sa steng gi ni skye bo rnams/_/rang gi gnas ni de nyid nas/_/'bebs shing 'gugs par byed pa'i mchog_/'di ni gshin rje brtan ma la/_/mos pa'i las rnams bshad pa yin/_/dbang po thams cad 'bebs pa yi/_gshin rje'i pho 
[\[87b.6\]]nya'i las bshad bya/_/ut+pal sngon po'i 'dab 'dra ba/_/mgo dang thig le spangs pa ni/_/ut+pal sngon po'i 'dab lta bu/_/gang gi lce dang rkan bsams na/_/skal ba bzang po 'joms par 'gyur/_/snying dang kha dang sna dang ni/_/mig dang rna bar de bzhin du/_/yi ge 
[\[87b.7\]]dbu ma bkod na ni/_/dbang po thams cad 'phrog 'gyur te/_/gzugs mi mthong zhing sgra mi thos/_/dri mi tshor zhing stobs mi 'gyur/_/de bzhin lce yis ro mi myong /_/dug gis brgyal ba bzhin du gnas/_/las rnams de dag thams cad la/_/yid kyis bzlog na grol 
[\[88a\]]
[\[88a.1\]]bar 'gyur/_/gshin rje mche ba du ba'i mdog_/sreg pa'i bskal ba lta bu ste/_/thams cad khyab par 'khyams 'gyur pa/_/sna tshogs 'od can bsgom par bya/_/drod kyi sde pa'i yi ge sa/_/du ba dang ni rlung gi mdog_/snying dang lag pa rkang par 
[\[88a.2\]]dgod/_/khams gsum yang ni bskrad 'gyur na/_/sa steng skye bo smos ci dgos/_/thig ler bcas shing ha dang ni/_/bcas pa gang gi lce steng du/_/bsams na phur bu lta bur 'gyur/_/yi ge dge ba de nyid ni/_/rtag tu lce steng bsams na ni/_/lan cig bzlas 
[\[88a.3\]]pas zin 'gyur te/_/de ni snyan dang gas mkhan por 'gyur/_/de ltar sa dang sa mtha' yi/_/yi ge'i cho ga bshad pa'o/_/'jig rten gsum po 'joms byed ma/_/'od zer dmar po gzi brjid che/_/nyi ma bye ba lta bu'i 'od/_/cung zad sngo ba 'jigs chen mo/_/ma 
[\[88a.4\]]he dang ni rta dag ni/_/'khrul 'khor sbyor ba kun la bsam/_/de yis yi ge'i rgyal po ni/_/gzi brjid chen po'i yi ge ha/_/de yi las ni sna tshogs mchog_/'dod pa'i las ni bshad par bya/_/de ltar bdag lus rtag tu ni/_/yi ge lngar ni bsam byas na/_/'gro 
[\[88a.5\]]ba rnams kyi bya ba ni/_/tshig gis thams cad byed par 'gyur/_/sa steng lha dang lha min dang /_/mi dang bcas pa 'bebs byed cing /_/sgrub pa po ni dus min par/_/'chi ba dag ni 'joms par byed/_/sgrub po yid la 'dod bzhin du/_/nad rnams thams cad gnon 
[\[88a.6\]]par byed/_/ngag tsam gyis ni dpung tshogs dang /_/chu dang rgya mtsho 'ching ba dang /_/gru dang khyogs dang 'khrul 'khor dang /_/rta dang glang po la sogs 'ching /_/shing chen sa la sgyel ba dang /_/sems can rnams ni dbang du byed/_/las kyi cho ga mthong ba yis/_/bya 
[\[88a.7\]]ba rnams ni 'dod bzhin byed/_/ha zhes bya ba'i yi ge mchog_/mgo dang bral zhing thig les brgyan/_/de yi las rnams ma lus pa/_/phan pa bshad kyis mnyan par gyis/_/khyab bya khyab byed stong pa ste/_/kun tu 'gro ba mi shigs pa/_/shin tu stong dang stong pa ste/_/
[\[88b\]]
[\[88b.1\]]de ni sems med gnyid log pa/_/sad pa dang ni bral ba ste/_/de ni ci yang snang mi 'gyur/_/lha dang lha min gnod sbyin min/_/mi'am ci dang dri za min/_/klu rnams dang ni srin po dang /_/rgya mtsho dang ni ri rab min/_/chu bo'i shing dang nags tshal dang /_/de bzhin sa sogs 
[\[88b.2\]]ma yin te/_/'jigs rung dmyal ba la sogs min/_/me dang du ba sogs min zhing /_/nyi ma zla ba gza' skar min/_/mi dang sha za la sogs min/_/mngal skyes dang ni drod las skyes/_/sgo nga dang ni rlan skyes min/_/thang la gnas dang chu gnas min/_/pad+ma las 
[\[88b.3\]]ni skyes pa dang /_/dbang po khyab 'jug bde byed min/_/rlung dang mkhris pa bde kan sogs/_/'byung ba'i tshogs la sogs min te/_/ha zhes bya ba yi ge mchog_/rtag tu bsams na dmigs mi 'gyur/_/lo gcig bar du bsgoms gyur na/_/rga dang nad kun 'joms 
[\[88b.4\]]'gyur te/_/de la dug kyang mi 'pho na/_/nad kyi 'jigs pa ga la zhig_/dug ni khur bu brgya zos kyang /_/de ni 'chi bar mi 'gyur ro/_/rtag tu stong nyid bsgoms na ni/_/de ni thar 'gyur the tshom med/_/mgo dang bral ba'i yi ge k+Sha/_/de nyid mchog tu thim nas su/_/
[\[88b.5\]]bsgoms nas skad cig mngon shes skye/_/thig ler ldan pa'i sbyor ba yis/_/rkan dang sna sgo'i lam du ni/_/ri mo dag ni zhugs pa bsam/_/ri mo mar me'i rtse lta bu/_/dkyil 'khor bzhi yi gzi can bsam/_/rtag tu rkan ni phug ste gnas/_/rtag tu sbyor bar gnas pa yis/_/
[\[88b.6\]]nyi ma bdun na mthong 'gyur te/_/'di dang mtshungs pa yod ma yin/_/bram ze gsod pa'ang nyin bdun gyis/_/rtse mo rgyas par mthong 'gyur te/_/de ltar mthong ba'i rnal 'byor pas/_/gzhan gyi grong sogs 'dod bzhin 'gro/_/k+Sha yi rgyal po 'di nyid kyi/_/las ni 
[\[88b.7\]]bsam gyis mi khyab gnas/_/'jig rten 'jig rten 'das dngos grub/_/brtul zhugs ma ni khyod la bshad/_/re re yi ni las rnams kyang /_/bstan pa ru ni nus ma yin/_/skye bo rang don byed pa yi/_/yid bzhin nor bu lta bu yin/_/yi ge sde tshan so so yi/_/yi ge 
[\[89a\]]
[\[89a.1\]]dag ni so so zhing /_/re re'ang sbyor ba bzhi bzhi ste/_/gcig kyang bzhi ru sbyor ba bzhin/_/A li kA li'i phyag rgya che/_/dngos grub thams cad rab ster ba/_/shes rab thabs ni ma gtogs par/_/go 'phang mchog ni 'thob mi 'gyur/_/sgyu ma che 
[\[89a.2\]]dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las/_/A li kA li'i sngags kyi sgrub thabs 'byung ba'i cho ga'i rim par phye ba ste 
[\[89a.3\]]bzhi bcu rtsa gcig pa'o//_//de nas bcom ldan 'das la lha mo rnams kyis mchod cing phyag 'tshal te/_slar gsol pa/_khyod kyis sngar bshad gsang ba dang /_/thams cad bdag nyid rtag tu bzhugs/_/yi ge bcu drug rig pa che/_/bde mchog 
[\[89a.4\]]bla mar bshad pa dang /_/khams rnams dang ni de bzhin rtsa/_/'byung gnas sgra tshul nges 'byung dang /_/'jig rten gsum du rnyed dka' ba/_/thog ma dbus mthar dbye ba da ga_/ji ltar nang du yang dag gnas/_/de rnams dbye ba de bzhin gsungs/_/bka' stsal pa/_
[\[89a.5\]]sgyu ma kun gyi rnam rol pa'i/_/nyon cig lha mo sgyu ma che/_/sangs rgyas kun la'ang gsang ba ste/_/gzhan rnams la lta smos ci dgos/_/sgyu ma chen po bcu drug ni/_/ma mo nyid du rab tu bshad/_/de yis nang gi dkyil 'khor du/_/gyur pa nyid du the tshom med/_/rnal 
[\[89a.6\]]'byor pa yi bdud rtsi sbyor/_/bcu drug char yang mi phod do/_/slar yang bdud rtsi rdzas rnams ni/_/da ni bshad kyis mnyan par gyis/_/ra ni dmar chen po yi khams/_/ha zhes bya ba ri las skyes/_/se ni gnad pa tra ste/_/pa ni pags par bstan pa yin/_/ra zhes 
[\[89a.7\]]bya ba dri ru gsungs/_/me zhes bya ba me tog ste/_/de bzhin ra ni chu yin no/_/me zhes bya ba pi si ta/_/sa ni kun du ru nyid yin/_/re ni sa ri zhes bya ste/_/At+ma zhes bya na la dza/_/ni zhes bya ba dri yi mar/_/sa ni so dang lag skyes yin/_/dA ni rgya mtsho las byung 
[\[89b\]]
[\[89b.1\]]ba/_/s+thi ni kha ba'i snying po yin/_/ta zhes bya ba ga bur te/_/byang chub sems las byung ba yi/_/rtag tu ye shes dam pa mchog_/rtsa rnams thams cad yang dag 'dus/_/de dag lha mo khyod la bstan/_/gsang sogs rnal 'byor pa yi rdzas/_/bshang dang gci sogs 
[\[89b.2\]]lnga la bya/_/khams rnams bsdus pa'i sde tshan ni/_/de ltar bcud len bstan pa yin/_/mkha' 'gro kun dngos chags pa yi/_/sems dpa' rdo rje las byung ba/_/pad+ma 'byed pa'i sbyor ba yis/_/pad+ma gar dbang zhes bya ste/_/steng du gsal bar 'phro bas na/_/rdo rje nyi 
[\[89b.3\]]ma zhes bya la/_/'di ni gsal zhing 'bar bas na/_/rnam snang mdzad chen thub pa'ang yin/_/rlung ni sna tshogs las byung ba/_/khyad par spyod pas rta mchog yin/_/rnam shes rdo rje sems dpa' ste/_/phan tshun gnyis med rang bzhin no/_/gnas der nyi ma nub pa yi/_/sprul ba'i 
[\[89b.4\]]sku yi gzugs kyi bdag_/lha dang mi yi bde ba ni/_/bsdus byas rnyog pa med pa ste/_/'khor ba'i lam ni gcod pa po/_/spros pa thams cad nyer zhi ba'i/_/rdo rje sems dpa' he ru ka/_/mkha' 'gro ma kun sgra ru bshad/_/yang na mkha' 'gro ma kun gyi sgras ni rab tu 
[\[89b.5\]]gtum mo la sogs pa la bya la dngos po ni rang bzhin te/_/de rnams kyi rdul phra rab las bsgrubs pa'o/_/de nyid sems can gyi don la nus pa dang ldan pa'i phyir sems dpa'i sgrar brjod de/_/mi shes pa'i ri bo 'joms pas na ye shes nyid ni rdo rje'o/_/sems dpa' rnam pa 
[\[89b.6\]]gnyis gsungs te/_/snod dang bcud kyi 'jig rten no/_/bcud ni rgyu ba zhes bshad la/_/de bzhin snod ni brtan pa'o/_/re re zhing yang gnyis gnyis te/_/skal ba bzang dang skal ngan no/_/sa dang chu dang me rlung gi/_/gzugs ni rnam pa bzhi ru gnas/_/'di ni zhes bya ba 
[\[89b.7\]]ni bcom ldan 'das de nyid de rang byung bas na rang byung bcom ldan 'das so/_/rang byung ba zhes bya ba ni 'byung ba chen po rjes su dpag pa'i rang bzhin gyi phyir ro/_/bcom ldan 'das zhes bya ba ni dbang phyug la sogs pa'i yon tan dang ldan pas so/_/yang na nyon mongs pa 
[\[90a\]]
[\[90a.1\]]la sogs pa'i bdud bcom pas na bcom ldan 'das te/_rang byung bcom ldan 'das de nyid lhag pa'i lha ni gcig nyid de lha nyid yin zhing lhag pa nyid kyi phyir lhag pa'i lha ste/_lhag pa'i lha yi sgrar gang sngar bstan pa'i khams rnams kyi nang na gtso bo dang dam par 
[\[90a.2\]]gyur pa ste/_phung po dang khams dang skye mched rnams yang dag par skyed par byed pa'i khu ba dang khrag go/_/mchog tu bde ba de las skyes shing byung bas na 'byung gnas te/_nges 'byung ni sems skyed pa'i bzhed gzhung gsungs pa'o/_/sgra ni dbyangs te/_shes rab kyi dbyangs 
[\[90a.3\]]kyi rjes su 'jug pa'o/_/tshul ni sgrar btags pa ste/_de las byung ba'o/_/bsten cing bsnyen par byas pas na dam tshig ste/_yul can gyis yang dag par bzhag pa'i gzugs la sogs pa'i yul rnams dang /_don dam par dbyer med pas nye bar brtags nas 
[\[90a.4\]]yul can dbang po la yang yul zhes bya'o/_/spyod pa ni dbang po bcu gnyis po de rnams kyi nye bar rgyu ba ste/_de rnams kyi rnam par shes pa'i rang bzhin byang chub kyi sems thob pa ni spyod yul lo/_/'jig rten gsum du rnyed dka' ba zhes bya ba ni zag pa dang bcas 
[\[90a.5\]]pa'i dngos po gsum las rnyed par mi 'gyur ba'i mtshan nyid de thog ma dbus mthar yang dag gnas pa'o/_/de la thog ma ni gsang ba ste/_'di skad ces bya ba sngar 'byung gnas su gyur pa'i rig pa'o/_/mtha' zhes bya ba ni dges ba ste thos ba zhes bya ba bsten par bya'o/_/
[\[90a.6\]]dbus zhes bya ba ni mchog ste/_bdag gi zhes bya ba shin tu dam par gyur pa'o/_/yang dag gnas ni thams cad bdag nyid de/_dus gcig na zhes bya ba'o/_/bcom ldan 'das ma sgyu ma chen mo de nyid ngan par spyod pa ste/_lo brgyad pa dang rdo rje pad+mo sbyor bas byung ba'i khrag 
[\[90a.7\]]dang bcas pa'o/_/lha dang mi yi bde ba ni/_/bsdus byas rdo rje rdul gyi ni/_/bcu drug char yang mi phod pa ste/_de la bcu drug cha ni bud med kyi skud pa las byung ba'i khams rnams skud pa bcu drug dang lhan cig tu bsgril ba ste chags pa chen po zhes bya ba gang yin pa'o/_/de ltar 
[\[90b\]]
[\[90b.1\]]skud pa bcu drug dang bsgril ba'i cha shas bcu drug go/_/bsdus byas zhes bya ba bdud rtsi lnga'i snying po'i gong bur byas pa ste/_bdud rtsi lnga yi sbyor bar gyur pa'o/_/dang zhes bya ba ni bsdu ba'i don te sde tshan rnams kyis bsdus shing bstan pa'i khams gang dang gang yin pa'i 
[\[90b.2\]]rlan de dang des bon dang bsgril te brtag par bya'o/_/lha'i bde ba zhes bya ba ni de bdud rtsi ltar yongs su smin pas rang gis sku rdo rjer gyur pa'i skad cig go/_/mi'i yin pas na mi yis ste nges par gsal ba'i bde ba'o/_/de yang dngos grub thams cad kyi rang bzhin rtag tu bde 
[\[90b.3\]]ba'i gsal bas mtshan pa'o/_/chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam par sbyor ba'i sems kyi rdo rje mngon du bya zhing bde ba bsten pa las bde ba nyid du skye bar 'gyur te/_ther zug tu bde ba ster bas so/_/de yang dper na mngar pos bsgos pa'i sa bon ni/_/mngar po nyid ni bsten pa 
[\[90b.4\]]dang /_/gang phyir mngar pos bskyed byas na/_/de yi ro ni mngar por 'gyur/_/de bzhin du chos kyi bdud rtsi las byung ba'i bde ba bsten na bde ba 'phel bar 'gyur ro/_/de dang mnyam pa dngos po gsum po gang na yang yod pa ma yin te cha shas kun dang mtshungs pa lta zhog_/de'i rdul 
[\[90b.5\]]phra rab kyi cha dang mtshungs pa yang yod pa ma yin no/_/rdul phra rab ni rdo rje phra mo'i rang bzhin te/_byang chub kyi sems bde ba'i bdag nyid spros pa'o/_/mthu dang ngo bo nyid dag dang /_/rang bzhin gyis ni grub pa ste/_/de yi rang bzhin gtam dag ni/_/bzhin mdzes ma ni khyod la bshad/_/
[\[90b.6\]]gang zhig phyi rol sbyor la dga'/_/bcu drug char yang mi phod do/_/sbyor ba'i mchog ni 'di mthong na/_/gzhan dag 'bus zos rtswa dang 'dra/_/da ni chu skyes chen po'i yon tan dang ldan pa'i gnas phun sum tshogs pa bshad par bya ste/_mi gsal brda yi tshul gyis ni/_/gnas 
[\[90b.7\]]ni gsang ba zhes byar bshad/_/mi gsal ba yi dngos por ni/_/brda yi skad kyis bshad pa yin/_/'di ni rtog pa la brten nas/_/gnas ni gsang ba bshad par bya/_/dgon chen zhes bya ba la sogs pa ni sgra ji bzhin no/_/gzhan yang dgon chen zhes bya ba ni shes rab ste/_
[\[91a\]]
[\[91a.1\]]sngags kyi yon tan gyi shing gi tshogs dpag tu med pa dang ldan pa'i phyir ro/_/de nyid rang bzhin gyis lha'i bdag nyid pas nas phyogs so/_/rigs mtha' yas pa'i rang bzhin gyi phyir mang po'i tshig go/_/bdag dang sems can la sogs par rtog pa dang bral bas 
[\[91a.2\]]rnam par dben pa ste/_/lta ba ngan par rnams kyis gzhig par mi nus pa'i phyir ri bo'o/_/'jug par dka' ba'i phyir ri 'or te/_zab cing rgya che ba'i shes rab kyi chu bo'i gnas su gyur pas rgya mtsho chen po'i 'gram rnams so/_/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad dang sbyin pa la 
[\[91a.3\]]sogs pa'i rang bzhin rnam par dag pa grub pas gdod nas grub pa ste/_nyan thos la sogs pa'i byang chub 'dod chags kyi mes bsregs pas dur khrod do/_/de ltar na me long lta bu la sogs pa lnga rnam par dag pas gnas rnam pa lngar bsgrags so/_/chung ngu ni dang por lo 
[\[91a.4\]]bcu gcig ma'i pad+ma dang rol pas bcud kyis len bsgrub par bya'o/_/gnyis par lo bcu gnyis ma dang rol pas ni dam tshig don du gnyer bar bya'o/_/gsum par lo bcu drug ma dang lhan cig tu dga' bas ni sngags rnams goms par bya'o/_/bzhi par lo nyi shu ma 
[\[91a.5\]]dang nam mkhar 'phur ba bsgrub par bya'o/_/lnga par lo nyi shu rtsa lnga ma dang dbang bskur bar bya'o/_/zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/de ltar na dbugs 'byung mchog dang bar ma dang /_zhes bya ba gnas lnga po de dag dang rim pa bzhin du sbyar ro/_/de la rigs ldan rnams ni 
[\[91a.6\]]rdo rje rigs kyi bu mo la sogs pa rnams dri yi chus mchod par bya ste/_bcas pa zhes bya ba ni rigs kyi bu mo de'i me tog bzung la mkhas pas rang gi khu ba dang bcas pas mchod do/_/rtag tu zhes bya ba ni thams cad du rgyun mi 'chad par te/_/dus kyi khyad par zhes bya ba snga dro'i 
[\[91a.7\]]dus su'o/_/gzhan yang lhan cig skyes grub ces bya ba thun mong gi dngos grub dang ldan zhing sgrub pa po la brtse ba ste/_/thig le des sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' rnams mchod par bya'o/_/gzhan yang dgon pa chen po zhes bya ba ni bud med kyi phyag rgya ste/_'dod chags 
[\[91b\]]
[\[91b.1\]]la sogs pa'i nyon mongs pa'i nags tshal dang ldan pa'i phyir ro/_/de nyid thabs kyis yongs su sbyangs na byang chub kyi lam ste/_yongs su sbyangs pa ni mthar thug par mon pa'o/_/de'i d+har mo da ya ni sa phyogs te/_de nyid de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis bsngags 
[\[91b.2\]]pa'i rdo rje rigs la sogs pa'i bu mo bas na rnam par dben pa'o/_/de nyid bde ba chen pos mi g.yo bas na ri bo'o/_/sems can dman pas 'jug par dka' bas na ri 'or te phug dang ldan pas na tshang tshing ngo /_/thabs dang shes rab snyoms par 'jug pa'i rgya mtsho'i gnas 
[\[91b.3\]]pas rgya mtsho'i 'gram ste/_sems can thams cad kyi rgyud la mnyam par gnas pas na dur khrod do/_/gdod nas grub pa ni rtag tu grub pa ste/_'char ba dang nub pa spangs shing nam mkha' ltar yongs su grub pas rtag tu bde ba chen po'i tshul gyis dmigs pas de'i rang 
[\[91b.4\]]bzhin du byas pa'o/_/ni zhes bya ba ni 'dus pa ste de dang ldan pa'i phyir ro/_/sna tshogs pad+ma ras kyis g.yogs zhes bya ba la sna tshogs ni rnam pa du ma ste/_pad+ma ni gzugs kyi tshig [{]bla dgas [,]bla dwags [}]so/_/ras ni gsal bar ma byas pa ste/_g.yogs pa ni shin tu mi mngon par byas 
[\[91b.5\]]ba'o/_/stan ni rig pa nyid gdan du byas pa ste dag pa ni 'jam pa'o/_/lha mo rnams kyi pad+ma rang gi mtshan ma'i gos rgyan gyis brgyan pa'o/_/gzhag pa ni mchod par bya ba ste/_yang dang yang du 'dod pas mnyes par bya ba'o/_/sdom pa ni bde ba'i mchog go/_/pad+ma'i 
[\[91b.6\]]khrag dang zhes bya ba ni de bzhin du khrag ni bcud kyis len pa ste/_khrag ni pad+ma yin la bshang ba la sogs pa ni bcud kyis len to/_/des 'di skad du bstan par 'gyur te/_khrag chen ga bur bcas pa dang /_/tsan+dan dmar por sbyar ba dag_/tshogs kyi nang du zhugs pa na/_/
[\[91b.7\]]ra sa ya na bla na med/_/ces bya ba la khrag chen ni dus su 'bab pa'i zla mtshan du gyur pa'o/_/tsan+dan dmar po ni sha ste de dang bcas pa'o/_/tshogs kyi nang du zhugs pa ni dbang po thams cad las byung ba ste/_/de dag thams cad kyi bcud ni ra sa ya na'o/_/bza' bar bya zhes 
[\[92a\]]
[\[92a.1\]]bya ba la/_cho ga gang gis bza' bar bya zhe na/_sbom po'am mtha' ltag ces bya ba la sbom po ni rdo rje'o/_/mtha' ltag ni b+ha ga ste/_a nA ma zhes bya ba ming gis bsnyad pa'i phyir ro/_/khar zhes bya ba ni de nyid kyi khas rang gi lha yi khar ro/_/de yang dang por bla 
[\[92a.2\]]mar dbul byas te/_/de nas sangs rgyas rnams la dbul/_/phyag rgya la ni byin nas su/_/zhi ba'i btung ba bzhin du myang /_/mchod pa bzhi zhes bya ba la/_/byang chub kyi sems bskyed pa'i rim pa sgom pa pos ni/_/dus bzhir mkha' 'gro ma la sogs pa'i lha mo dang /_
[\[92a.3\]]rnam par thar pa'i sgo bzhi'i bdag nyid can ji srid stong pa nyid kyi nga rgyal nas bzung ste/_gzugs yongs su rdzogs pa'i bar du nges par byas pas mchod par bya'o/_/dpa' bo dang ni snyoms 'jug pa zhes bya ba ni/_gzhan yongs su rdzogs pa'i rim pa la gnas pa'i 
[\[92a.4\]]sgom pa po'i ste/_dus kyi nges pa med cing thog ma dang tha ma med pa'i nga rgyal gyis rtag tu gnas pa rnams kyis ci zhig bya zhe na/_yid ni nang du chud pa'i ste/_de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi skyed ma mo'i shes rab dang ji lta ba bzhin du mnyam par sbyor ba dang ldan pas 
[\[92a.5\]]mchog tu dga' ba rnam par spro ste/_gnas pas tshim par bya zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/de ltar mchod pas tha ma mchog dang bar ma ste/_tha ma ni chags pa chen po'i tshul lo/_/de yang rdo rje can gyis thams cad bsdus nas btsun mo'i b+ha ga bde ba can du spros la/_der 
[\[92a.6\]]ni dkyil 'khor bri bar bya zhes bya ba dang sbyar ba ste/_gzhan gyi de kho na nyid ji lta ba bzhin du bstan pa'o/_/bar ma ni bdag gi de kho na nyid de/_rdo rje 'dzin pas 'khor lo thams cad rang gi dbus su bsdus la/_rdo rje kun gyi rang bzhin du byas pa cho ga des bsgoms pa ni mchog 
[\[92a.7\]]go/_/bud med kun gyi sgyu ma zhes bya ba la bud med kun ni gzugs la sogs pa'o/_/sgyu ma ni yon tan te rang bzhin gyis sku la sogs pas gnas pa'o/_/sgyu ma ste yon tan gyi sgyus ma lus pa/_sgyu ma'i don du gsungs so/_/ma lus pa ni cha shas te cha dang bcas pa'o/_/
[\[92b\]]
[\[92b.1\]]cha ni dngos por bstan pa ste/_/cha med yon tan zhes bya yin/_/yon tan yon tan can sbyar ba/_/yul dang dbang po rab tu dge/_/A li kA li yon tan gsungs/_/mnyam pa dam tshig spyod yul yin/_/de nyid ni mtshan ma yin pas na phyag rgya ste/_/'di ni zhes bya ba ni 
[\[92b.2\]]'dzin pa po'i blos mngon du byas pa'o/_/yon tan des rtag tu yon tan dang ldan par gyur pa'i ring po la sogs pa gnyis su med pa'i tshul nyid mchog ste/_bla na med cing dam par gyur pa'o/_/de yang lugs ma la sogs pa gzhan dag las phul du byung bar rang bzhin 
[\[92b.3\]]gyis grub pa'i phyir ro/_/rang bzhin gyis grub pa ni 'gyur ba med pa'i mtshan nyid dang ngo bo nyid rnam par dag pa ste/_ji ltar 'o ma la zho la sogs pa bzhin no/_/de bas na bcos ma las bye brag tu gyur pa'o/_/gzhan yang ngan par spyod pa'ang 'grub par 'gyur/_rnyed dang 
[\[92b.4\]]bde dang dga' kun gyis/_/zhes bya ba la rnyed pa ni byang chub kyi sems rnyed pa ste/_de nyid dga' ba kun gyis khyad par du 'phel bas yin gyi/_slongs mos 'tsho ba la sogs pa dang /_dka' thub kyi sbyor ba la sogs pa rnams ni mi mthun pa'i phyogs yin pas nam 
[\[92b.5\]]yang 'grub par mi 'gyur te spang bar bya'o/_/des zhes bya ba ni grub pa'i rdzas kyis te/_khyad par ji lta bu zhig ce na/_rang bzhin dang ngo bo nyid kyis grub pa ste/_rang bzhin gyi sngags kyi yi ge yongs su gyur pa las rang gi lha'i gzugs su yongs su brje ba ste/_sngags 
[\[92b.6\]]bzlas pa dang /_rang gi sa bon yongs su gyur pa las rang bzhin yongs su brje ba'o/_/sna tshogs zhes bya ba ni khams kyi rang bzhin te/_mkha' 'gro ma rnam pa [{]bcwa [,]bco [}]brgyad du phye ba yin gyi/_b+hu ba la sogs pa'i khams ni ma yin no/_/nam mkha' kun tu rgyu grub pa/_/
[\[92b.7\]]zhes bya ba la nam mkha'i sgras ni sems la bya ste/_ji ltar nam mkha'i khams la sogs pa thams cad kyi gnas su gyur pa bzhin du thams cad kyi go skabs ster ba'i phyir ro/_/yang na nyan thos la sogs pa'i gnas bsregs pas nam mkha' ste/_nam mkha' der bdud rtsi'i 
[\[93a\]]
[\[93a.1\]]rang bzhin gyis rgyu zhing spyod pas na/_de ni nam mkha' kun tu rgyu grub pas/_/mkha' 'gro ma zhes rab tu grags zhes so/_/sna tshogs kun tu phyag rgya ste zhes bya ba la/_/kun tu ni sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje'i rigs thams cad do/_/sna tshogs ni rdo rje 
[\[93a.2\]]'dzin chen po ste/_de'i phyag rgya ni dkyil 'khor gyi 'khor lo'i phyag rgya thams cad do/_/des na sna tshogs phyag rgya ste/_sems kyi rang bzhin gyis zla ba dang dmar po 'grub pa'i rdzas de dag nyi shu rtsa lnga'i bar du rtag tu goms par bya bas stobs su gyur pa'o/_/sna tshogs sdom 
[\[93a.3\]]pa ni rnam pa sna tshogs pa'i bde ba grub pa ste/_mkha' 'gro kun zhes bya ba khrag dang mnyam par sbyor ba'i cho gas sngags pa rdo rje mkha' 'gro zhes bya bar grags pa ste/_rang nyid skyes par gyur pa'o/_/de nyid med sems ni de kho na nyid kyi sems med pa ste/_phyin ci 
[\[93a.4\]]log gi sems kyis sbyor ba'o/_/lha la dmigs pa ni 'dod pa'i lhar sgom pa'o/_/shin tu de nyid sems zhes bya ba ni de kho na nyid kyi sems kyis brgyan pa ste/_phyin ci ma log pa'i mtshan nyid do/_/de yang khrag la sogs pa'i khams sangs rgyas rnams kyi rang 
[\[93a.5\]]bzhin gyi de kho na nyid de dngos grub thams cad kyi gnas so/_/lugs ma la sogs pa'i rnal 'byor ni de spangs nas bcos ma sgom par byed pa ste/_gang gi phyir gzugs brnyan ma yin pas so/_/des ni 'di skad bstan par 'gyur te/_ji ltar smin pa'i 'bras 
[\[93a.6\]]bu bor nas/_shing gi rtsa ba len par byed pa bzhin du/_rnal ma bor te bcos ma la chags pa yang blo gros dman zhing shin tu blun par ston to/_/des na de kho na nyid kyi sems kyis phun sum tshogs pas bdag nyid sbyor zhing rdzogs pa'i rim pas sangs rgyas kyi sku 
[\[93a.7\]]la dmigs pa ni mchog tu mi 'gyur ba'i ye shes 'grub par 'gyur ro/_/ye shes de nyid ni mchog tu mi 'gyur ba ste/_thog ma dang tha ma med pa'i phyir ro/_des na gzhan yang gzugs brnyan dang lugs ma la sogs pa spangs te/_khams sna tshogs kyi shes pa nyid 
[\[93b\]]
[\[93b.1\]]sangs rgyas thams cad 'dus par brtag par bya'o/_/lha yi rnal 'byor dus su ni/_/ba spu rnams kyi bu ga nas zhes bya ba la ba spu ni lte ba'i dkyil 'khor te bu ga ni nam mkha'o/_/rtse mo'i bar zhes bya ba ni yi ge bcu drug yongs su gyur pa'i khams rnams te/_spro ba dam tshig 
[\[93b.2\]]dkyil 'khor te/_/rnal 'byor pas ni rtag tu bsgom/_/dam tshig dkyil 'khor zhes bya ba/_/rang gi pad+ma'i lte dbus su/_/zla bar gnas pa'i hU~M yin te/_/sangs rgyas bye ba stong phrag ni/_/phyogs bcu dag tu spro bar byed/_/thig le bcu drug thig skud kyi/_/sngags 
[\[93b.3\]]pas thig gis btab pa yi/_/lte ba'i dkyil 'khor bcu drug par/_/a sogs k+Sha yi mthar thug pa/_/tha ma las ni sngags btu bya/_/phyi nas thig ler cha dang bral/_/bshang dang gci ba'i rang bzhin pas/_/'di ru k+Sha ni spang bar bya/_/yi ge ha ni yongs gyur 
[\[93b.4\]]pas/_/yi ge thams cad yal ga 'dra/_/'di ni rang byung bcom ldan 'das/_/gcig pu nyid ni lhag pa'i lha/_/sangs rgyas kun dngos stong pa yi/_/yi ge nyid ni mkha' 'gro ma/_/zhes bya ba ni sngags kyi man ngag go/_/ka sogs yang dag gnas pa yi/_/yi 
[\[93b.5\]]ge lha yi gzugs yin te/_/a sogs dbyangs rnams thams cad kyi/_/sngags kyis sgeg cing brgyan pa yi/_/lhag pa'i lha ni yi ge hU~M/_/lte ba'i dbus su bsam par bya/_/lte ba pad+ma zhes bya ste/_/bsam pa bsgom par bya ba yin/_/sangs rgyas kun dngos 
[\[93b.6\]]stong pa ni/_/rnam shes hU~M gi gzugs nyid do/_/yi ge nyid ni mkha' 'gro ma/_/sgra yi gzugs kyis gnas pa'o/_/gang phyir gzhan yang 'di dag ni/_/ji ltar sangs rgyas kun de bzhin/_/'di ni rtag tu 'don pa dang /_/sangs rgyas kun gyi thugs la gnas/_/
[\[93b.7\]]mgo dang thig le rnam spangs pa/_/gcig pu nyid ni lhag pa'i lha/_/rang gi dbus gnas rdo rje can/_/yi ge rnams kyis yongs su bskor/_/de kho na nyid snang ba dam pa'i yul la 'jug pa zhes bya ba la ye shes ni spyod yul yongs su shes pa'o/_/yongs su shes pa zhes bya ba ni kun 
[\[94a\]]
[\[94a.1\]]nas shes pa'i ye shes te/_de nyid kyis ni bdag nyid sangs rgyas thams cad do/_/bdag nyid ces bya ba ni kun rdzob ste/_kun rdzob yongs su shes pa ni sangs rgyas nyid do/_/de nyid kyis sangs rgyas dang rdo rje 'dzin pa nyid du 'grub bo/_/thabs gang gis zhe na de kho 
[\[94a.2\]]na nyid snang ba zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro bde ba'i mchog_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las gsang ba zhes bya ba la 
[\[94a.3\]]sogs pa de kho na nyid 'byung ba'i rim par phye ba ste bzhi bcu rtsa gnyis pa'o//_//sbyin sreg las kyi cho ga ni/_/ji bzhin bshad kyis lha mo nyon/_/rgyal srid phyir ni sngags pa yis/_/stong phrag bcur ni bzla bar bya/_/pho nya'i steng du gnas nas ni/_/sbyin sreg 
[\[94a.4\]]stong phrag lnga ni bya/_/sha chen 'o ma la bsgres pa/_/rim pa 'di yis bsregs na ni/_/dpag tshad 'bum phrag brgya yi bar/_/tshun chad pho brang rnyed par 'gyur/_/dpal ldan rgyal srid che grub pa/_/sus kyang gzhig par mi nus so/_/mi dang ba lang ce spyang shas/_/de bzhin 
[\[94a.5\]]ngo mtshar mchog 'gyur te/_/chang dang 'o mar sbags nas ni/_/sbyin sreg 'bum phrag gcig byas na/_/gling bdun la ni dbang ba yi/_/rgyal srid longs spyod dpal chen 'gyur/_/de nas mtshams med lnga byed pa/_/gang gis gzhom par 'dod pa des/_/dug dang khrag 
[\[94a.6\]]dang mar nag bcas/_/mi yi rus pa bsregs byas te/_/tsher ma can gyi shing dang ni/_/de bzhin phub ma mi skrar bcas/_/shan pa yi ni me sbar la/_/gcer bu skra ni lhug pa yis/_/lho phyogs su ni kha bltas te/_/khro dang bcas pas nyin phyed na/_/sems mi 
[\[94a.7\]]g.yeng bas bsreg pa'am/_/yang na gos sngon gyon nas su/_/mtshan phyed na ni bsreg par bya/_/sngags kyi mthar ni ming dang sbyar/_/dgra bo stobs dang ldan pa yang /_/dpung dang bcas par brlag 'gyur na/_/phal pa la lta ci zhig smos/_/de nas dgra bo 
[\[94b\]]
[\[94b.1\]]stobs kyis ni/_/dregs pa bskrad pa'i cho ga bshad/_/khwa yi gshog pa zhag dang ni/_/ni ma pa'i khu bar bsres pa dag_/sha za'i shing la me sbar te/_/rlung du bltas te bsreg byas na/_/mtshan mo bdun ni tshun chad kyis/_/bskrad 'gyur 'dir ni the tshom med/_/de nas 
[\[94b.2\]]dbye ba bshad par bya/_/smyo byed me ni 'bar ba la/_/ni ma pa yi ni lo ma dang /_/sbrul gyi pags par bsre byas la/_/bya rog dang ni 'ug pa yi/_/tshang gi shing dang bcas pa dag_/brgya rtsa brgyad du bsregs na ni/_/mdza' bshes gnyen 'dun bcas pa dang /_/
[\[94b.3\]]skye bo thams cad sdang bar 'gyur/_/de nas dgug pa bshad bya ste/_/bsgrub bya shing gi stan la ni/_/rol par bcas shing g.yo dang bcas/_/rlung gis gdong ni steng du blta/_/li khri lta bu'i mdog tu bsam/_/zhags pa lcags kyu'i sbyor ba yis/_/sngags kyi mthar 
[\[94b.4\]]ni dza bzlas te/_/bsgrub bya'i snying gi pad+ma dbug_/bud med yin na skye gnas dbug_/khams gsum 'grub par gdon mi za/_/de nas cho ga gzhan bshad pa/_/dgug pa la sogs ji bzhin 'byung /_/dum bu gcig pa'i thod pa ni/_/gzugs mdzes ma chag ma 
[\[94b.5\]]gsal/_/bsgrub par bya ba'i gzugs bris te/_/'dab ma rlon pa brgya la ni/_/khrag dang go ro tsa nar bcas/_/d+ha du ra yi smyug gu yis/_/sngags ni ming dang bcas par bri/_/thod pa'i nang du bzhag nas su/_/bsgrub bya dmar po ras kyis g.yogs/_/sngags kyi nga rgyal 
[\[94b.6\]]bsgyings nas su/_/'dab ma re re bzung nas ni/_/go bzlog snying po'i gsang sngags ni/_/zlos shing 'dab ma thams cad bsreg_/yid la bsams bzhin 'gugs par 'gyur/_/de nas gzhan yang tsan+da na ni/_/dmar las bsgrub bya'i gzugs byas la/_/rang khrag go ro tsa 
[\[94b.7\]]nar bcas/_/ming bris bsgrub bya'i snying gar gzhug_/tsha ba gsum gyis byugs nas su/_/snying ga lte ba gsang ba'i gnas/_/zangs ma'i khab kyis dbug byas na/_/mtshan phyed na ni 'dod bzhin 'gugs/_/gser btsag gis ni skye gnas kyi/_/gzugs bris de yi 
[\[95a\]]
[\[95a.1\]]steng du ni/_/lag pa g.yon pa gzhag byas la/_/brgya rtsa brgyad du bzlas na ni/_/gang gi ming nas bzung gyur pa/_/de ni de mod 'ong bar 'gyur/_/rlung gis gtsubs pa'i shing lo la/_/khwa yi sgro yi smyug gu yis/_/ming dang bcas pa'i 'khrul 'khor ni/_/
[\[95a.2\]]mchi ma las ni bris nas su/_/gyen du ldang ba'i tshub mar bskur/_/cho ga des ni bskrad par 'gyur/_/spre'u'i rus pa'i phur bu ni/_/sor drug pa yi tshad du bya/_/sngags ni lan bdun bzlas nas su/_/gang gi khyim sgor sbas na ni/_/de yi rigs ni chad par 'gyur/_/ba glang rta 
[\[95a.3\]]dang glang chen dang /_/bong bu rnga mo ma he yi/_/gnas su sbas na brlag par 'gyur/_/rlung gi dkyil 'khor la gnas pa'i/_/bsgrub bya rnga mo'i steng du bskyon/_/gcer bu mar khus byugs pa dang /_/skra grol kha ni lho ru bltas/_/skyabs med ngu zhing brgyal bar bsam/_/mgrin pa 
[\[95a.4\]]zhags pas bcings nas su/_/rgyab nas khro bos rdeg byed cing /_/khams gsum gnon pa'i bsam pa yis/_/me yi mtshams su gdong bltas te/_/sngags kyi mthar ni dza bzlas na/_/ji ltar 'dod bzhin 'grub par 'gyur/_/rta dang ma her zhon pa yi/_/dgra bo nang ni 
[\[95a.5\]]phan tshun du/_/mtshon cha thogs nas khros nas ni/_/phan tshun dpung dang bcas pa ru/_/'thab mo byed pa bsgom par bya/_/sngags pa g.yul du kha bltas te/_/de yi ming dang spel ba yi/_/yi ge ra ni bri bar bya/_/gdong ni steng du blta bar bsam/_/sgrub po ro yi 
[\[95a.6\]]steng du ni/_/rdo rje skyil krung mi g.yor gnas/_/de yis bsgrub bya mnan nas su/_/lus dang ngag ni gzir bar bya/_/sngags ni ming dang spel ba ru/_/snga ma bzhin du bzla bar bya/_/bcing ba'i cho ga'o/_/mkha' 'gro kun dang mnyam sbyor ba'i/_/rdo rje mkha' 'gro 
[\[95a.7\]]bde ba'i mchog_/rdo rje sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs/_/bcom ldan rdo rje can gyis gsungs/_/rgyud kyi rgyal po chen po dpal rdo rje mkha' 'gro las las thams cad kyi bsam gtan 'byung ba dang sbyin sreg gi cho ga'i rim par phye ba ste bzhi bcu rtsa gsum pa'o//_//
[\[95b\]]
[\[95b.1\]]da ni sbyin sreg cho ga gzhan/_/rab tu bshad kyis lha mo nyon/_/bsgegs pa'am ni gzhan yang rung /_/rma med shin tu mdzes pa ni/_/'byung po'i nyin zhag la rnyed pa/_/sgrub pos sngar bshad bzhin bsgrubs na/_/dngos grub thams cad rab tu 'grub/_/zla mtshan phyis 
[\[95b.2\]]pa'i ras ma ni/_/byi la'i rtug par bcas pa dag_/zhib tu brdungs pas bdug pa bya/_/bdug pa 'di yis sgrub pa po/_/shin tu byin gyis rlob par 'gyur/_/khyi mo nag mo'i 'o ma dang /_/ma sha 'bras bu bcas pa las/_/'o thug byas te ro yi khar/_/'jigs pa 
[\[95b.3\]]med par sbyin sreg bya/_/de nas ro de langs nas ni/_/ci bgyi zhes ni smra 'gyur te/_/ril bu mig sman dngos grub dang /_/rkang byug ral gri de bzhin te/_/gter dang dgra bo tshar gcod dang /_/gnod sbyin mo dang bcud kyis len/_/gang bslangs gcig ni byin nas 
[\[95b.4\]]su/_/'gro bar 'gyur gyi gzhan du min/_/dpa' bo rnams dang rtse 'gyur ba/_/gzhan dag tu ni nga mi smra/_/de nas ro langs chen po yi/_/sgrub thabs gzhan ni bshad par bya/_/sngar bstan mtshan nyid ldan pa yi/_/ro ni mdzes pa bzung nas ni/_/rnal 'byor chen 
[\[95b.5\]]por legs gnas pa/_/mtshan phyed na ni bsgoms nas su/_/de yi sha ni dmar chen bcas/_/sbyin sreg brgya rtsa brgyad byas na/_/dpal ldan rdo rje phag mo ni/_/mkha' 'gro ma yis bskor gyur cing /_/ro langs srin po du mas bskor/_/ha har bzhad pas 'jigs 
[\[95b.6\]]byed pa/_/der ni drung du 'ong bar 'gyur/_/ce spyang brgya phrag mang po ni/_/bskor nas 'jigs pa'i sgra sgrogs byed/_/de mthong 'jigs par mi bya ste/_/sngags kyis bsgyings pa'i lus su bya/_/rdo rje brtul zhugs bdag nyid sems/_/kye ho dpa' bo chen po legs/_/

[\[96a.1\]]tu rgyu bar 'gyur/_/dpa' bo 'dod pa'i gzugs can te/_/skal bzang de bzhin gshegs pa 'dra/_/rtag tu nam mkha' 'phur ba dang /_/rga dang 'chi ba rnam par spong /_/sa gzhi kun tu rgyu 'gyur zhing /_/ji ltar 'dod bzhin dngos grub 'jug_/de nas nyon

[\[97a\]]
[\[97a.1\]]la khyad par du/_/mdor bsdus nas ni bstan pa yin/_/zhi ba la ni mdzub mor bzhag_/rgyas la gung mor dgal bar bya/_/dbang du bya ba srin lag gsungs/_/mtha' ma la ni mngon spyod bya/_/mthe bo lcags kyur byas nas ni/_/lha rnams thams cad

[\[97b.1\]]bas kun nas 'khrigs par bsgom/_/ba~M las byung ba'i mi sdug gzugs/_/sdig ni sna yi sgo nas dbyung /_/de nas khro bo'i gzugs bsgoms la/_/de ni kun nas za bar bsgom/_/oM badzra DA ki nI kha kha khA hi khA hi/_/sarba pA pa~M da ha da ha b+ha smi~M ku ru hU~M hU~M phaT/_bsgrub

[\[98a.1\]]par bsam/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]rdo leb gzhi dang ni/_/gtsug lag khang dang zhabs kyis bcag_/ba glang lhas dang rgya mtsho'i 'gram/_/lag pa sa la bzhag nas su/_/rang gi snying po brgyad brgyar bzla/_/der ni brko sogs bya mi dgos/_/de ltar sbyangs pa'i

[\[109a.1\]]de nyid kyis bya'i gdong pa'i rtsa'i bdud rtsis yon tan de yang dang yang du goms par bya bas so/_/byis pa zhes bya ba ni shed ma bye ba ste/_bu mo gzhon nu ma'o/_/nyi shu zhes bya ba lo nyi shu'i bar du'o/_/de rnams thabs kyis sbyang bar bya zhes bya ba ni de rnams nyid 
[\[109a.2\]]sa bon gdab pa'i zhing du gyur pas sbyang bar bya ba yin la/_de rnams nyid thabs yin te mngon par 'dod pa'i don nye bar gtod cing ster la 'bras bu mngon sum du byed pa zhes bya ba'i bar du'o/_/phra mo ni yid du 'ong ba ste nam mkha' lta bu'i spyod yul lo/_/stobs ldan gzung ba yi ni

[\[109a.4\]]'dogs par byed pas na yongs su sbyong ba'o/_/kun gab ces bya ba ni srog chags ne'u le ltar kun du 'khyud pa dang /_phug ron ltar 'o byed cing sos ma mchu bzung ste 'jib pa dang skra nas gzung ba dang /_sen mo 'debs pa dang khu tshur snun pa dang /_sos 'debs shing 'dral ba dang mchan 
[\[109a.5\]]khung dang nu ma dang /_rked pa dang brla gnyis dang mgrin pa dang rgyab tu sen mos 'dral zhing sid ces bya ba'i sgra dang bcas par sngags pas sngon du bya zhing /_/gzhan yang nu ma mnye ba la sogs pas de'i sems rnam par 'dul ba'i bya ba de dag so sor byed pa'i tshul rnam pa brgyad dang sbyar 
[\[109a.6\]]ro/_/sen mo 'debs pa de yang 'jam zhing gzhon nu la ni ku sha'i tshad tsam du gdab pa dang dral bar byas te/_rked pa dang rgyab dang b+ha ga'i steng dang skra dang 'gram pa la sogs pa rnams su 'bras kyi gzegs ma'i tshad tsam gyi ri mo ci rigs par chags pa lhod pa rnams la gdab bo//

[\[109b.1\]]mo la sogs pa tshim par bya ba ni rdo rje sor mo zhes bya'o/_/gang zhig gzhon zhing mnyen pa rnams la ni rang gi mtshan ma dang 'od dang ldan pa'i byang chub kyi sems 'bab pa'i rtsar lce bcug ste bsgril ba tsam gyis dga' bar bya ste rdo rje lce'i thur ma zhes bya'o/_/de ltar sgrub pa pos yang

[\[109b.3\]]par sbyor bas do la dang /_kur+b+ba Na dang /_/su tsi dang ud k+She pa dang su pra sa ri dang /_gra ma nA ga ra dang /_preng khe na dang u ru spo da zhes bya ba la sogs pa ji snyed bstan bcos thams cad las 'byung ba'i kar+Na bstan par bya ste/_de dag kun kyang rdo rje sems dpa'i phyag rgyar brjod do/_/de

[\[110a.1\]]la lta bar byed pa'o/_/de yang 'dir phyag rgya'i ye shes kyi dbang bskur bas dbugs phyung ba yan la/_phyag rgya'i ye shes kyang rnam pa gnyis te/_dngos grub sna tshogs dang bde ba 'jug pas rig pa dang rig par bya bar gzung ngo /_/de ltar na dbang po kun las

[\[113a.1\]]ge sar dang /_/u shi ra dang na le sham/_/glang po che yi pi pi ling /_/bu ram srang ni gcig dang bcas/_/chu ni srang bdun nang du blugs/_/rgya spos brag zhun ta ma la/_/rgya spos ru rta cha re bya/_/'bru chang bre gang nang du ni/_/thams cad bcas te nyin bdun

[\[113b.1\]]phabs ni bcos pa'i cho ga yin/_/peSh+thi las byung myos byed de/_/zan las byung ba chu can mchog_/tsu ta la sogs shun pa las/_/byung ba chang zhes bya ba yin/_/dza ga la ni 'bru chang brjod/_/rgun 'brum sbrang rtsi dpa' bo gcig_/skyur po g.yon can zan mo yin/_/tsin tsa rtsa ba las
